task = v2
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
local tbl = {FPSBooster = false}
local tbl2 = {connections = {fpsBooster = nil}, originalSettings = nil}

local function fn()
    tbl.FPSBooster = true
    local terrain = Workspace.Terrain

    if not tbl2.originalSettings then
        tbl2.originalSettings = {
            GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
            WaterTransparency = terrain.WaterTransparency,
            WaterWaveSize = terrain.WaterWaveSize,
            WaterWaveSpeed = terrain.WaterWaveSpeed,
        }
    end

    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.Brightness = 0
    terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
    terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
    terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0

    for i, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("Fire") or descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
            descendant.Enabled = false
        end

        if descendant:IsA("Decal") then
            local flag = false
            local parent = descendant.Parent

            if parent and parent.Name == "RespawnLocation" then
                flag = true
            end

            if not flag then
                if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                    descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalTexture", descendant.Texture)
                end

                descendant.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
            end
        end

        if descendant:IsA("Texture") then
            if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalTexture", descendant.Texture)
            end

            descendant.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
        end

        if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("UnionOperation") or descendant:IsA("Part") then
            if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalMaterial") then
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalMaterial", descendant.Material.Name)
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalReflectance", descendant.Reflectance)
            end

            descendant.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            descendant.Reflectance = 0
        end
    end

    if tbl2.connections.fpsBooster then
        tbl2.connections.fpsBooster:Disconnect()
    end

    tbl2.connections.fpsBooster = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if not tbl.FPSBooster then
            return
        end

        if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("Fire") or descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
            descendant.Enabled = false
        end

        if descendant:IsA("Decal") then
            local flag = false
            local parent = descendant.Parent

            if parent and parent.Name == "RespawnLocation" then
                flag = true
            end

            if not flag then
                if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                    descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalTexture", descendant.Texture)
                end

                descendant.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
            end
        end

        if descendant:IsA("Texture") then
            if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalTexture", descendant.Texture)
            end

            descendant.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
        end

        if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("UnionOperation") or descendant:IsA("Part") then
            if not descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalMaterial") then
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalMaterial", descendant.Material.Name)
                descendant:SetAttribute("OriginalReflectance", descendant.Reflectance)
            end

            descendant.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            descendant.Reflectance = 0
        end
    end)
end

local function fn2()
    tbl.FPSBooster = false

    if tbl2.connections.fpsBooster then
        tbl2.connections.fpsBooster:Disconnect()
        tbl2.connections.fpsBooster = nil
    end

    if tbl2.originalSettings then
        local terrain = Workspace.Terrain
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = tbl2.originalSettings.GlobalShadows
        Lighting.Brightness = tbl2.originalSettings.Brightness
        terrain.WaterTransparency = tbl2.originalSettings.WaterTransparency
        terrain.WaterWaveSize = tbl2.originalSettings.WaterWaveSize
        terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = tbl2.originalSettings.WaterWaveSpeed

        for i, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("Fire") or descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
                descendant.Enabled = true
            end

            if descendant:IsA("Decal") and descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                descendant.Texture = descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture")
            end

            if descendant:IsA("Texture") and descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture") then
                descendant.Texture = descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalTexture")
            end

            if (descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("UnionOperation") or descendant:IsA("Part")) and descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalMaterial") then
                descendant.Material = Enum.Material[descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalMaterial")]
                local attribute = descendant:GetAttribute("OriginalReflectance")
                local reflectance = attribute or 0
                descendant.Reflectance = reflectance
            end
        end

        tbl2.originalSettings = nil
    end
end

local flag = false
local clockTime = 12
local connection = nil

local function fn3()
    if connection then
        connection:Disconnect()
    end

    connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if flag then
            Lighting.ClockTime = clockTime
        end
    end)
end

local function fn4()
    if connection then
        connection:Disconnect()
        connection = nil
    end
end

local function fn5()
    local character_ = localPlayer.Character
    local torso = character_

    if character_ then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        torso = humanoidRootPart_ or character_:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    end

    return torso
end

local function fn6()
    return Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
end

local function fn7(arg, arg2)
    local menuToys = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
    local spawnToyRemoteFunction = menuToys and menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")

    if spawnToyRemoteFunction then
        pcall(function()
            spawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg, arg2, Vector3.zero)
        end)

        local v3 = fn6()

        if v3 then
            local v4 = v3:WaitForChild(arg, 3)
            local v5 = v4 or v3:FindFirstChild(arg)
            return v5
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function fn8(arg)
    local menuToys = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
    local destroyToy = menuToys and menuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")

    if arg and destroyToy then
        pcall(function()
            destroyToy:FireServer(arg)
        end)
    end
end

local function fn9(arg)
    local v3 = fn5()
    local grabEvents_ = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
    local setNetworkOwner = grabEvents_ and grabEvents_:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")

    if v3 and setNetworkOwner then
        pcall(function()
            setNetworkOwner:FireServer(arg, v3.CFrame)
        end)
    end
end

local function fn10()
    local v3 = fn5()
    local character_ = localPlayer.Character
    if not v3 or not character_ then
        return
    end
    local cFrame = v3.CFrame
    local InstrumentWoodwindOcarina = fn7("InstrumentWoodwindOcarina", v3.CFrame)
    if not InstrumentWoodwindOcarina then
        return
    end
    local tbl3 = {InstrumentWoodwindOcarina, character_}
    local holdPart = InstrumentWoodwindOcarina:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart and holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")

    if holdItemRemoteFunction then
        pcall(function()
            holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(unpack(tbl3))
        end)
    end

    task.wait(0.05)
    v3.CFrame = CFrame.new(304.06, 25.77, 488.54)
    task.wait(0.05)
    fn8(InstrumentWoodwindOcarina)
    task.wait(0.05)
    v3.CFrame = cFrame
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

    if plots then
        local v4, v5, v6 = ipairs(plots:GetChildren())
        local v7 = v6
        local v8 = v4
        local v9 = v5

        while true do
            local v10, v11 = v8(v9, v7)
            if v10 == nil then
                break
            end
            v7 = v10
            local barrier = v11:FindFirstChild("Barrier")

            if barrier then
                for i, child in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                        child.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end

                v7 = v10
            end
        end
    end

    task.wait(1)
    local InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2 = fn7("InstrumentWoodwindOcarina", CFrame.new(264.767242, -7.350404, 428.284058, 0.901897, -0.0, 0.43195, -0.0, 1, 0, -0.43195, -0.0, 0.901897))

    if InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2 then
        local holdPart2 = InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
        local soundPart = holdPart2 or InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") or InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2:FindFirstChild("Handle") or InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2.PrimaryPart

        if soundPart then
            fn9(soundPart)
            task.wait(0.5)
            soundPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(304, 25, 488)
            task.wait(0.75)
        end

        fn8(InstrumentWoodwindOcarina2)
    end

    if Rayfield and Rayfield.Notify then
        Rayfield:Notify({
            Title = "Success",
            Content = "Break Barrier が実行されました",
            Duration = 3,
            Image = 4483362458,
        })
    end
end

LocaleToCountry = {
    ["en-us"] = "🇺🇸 USA",
    ["en-gb"] = "🇬🇧 UK",
    ["ko-kr"] = "🇰🇷 Korea",
    ["zh-cn"] = "🇨🇳 China",

}

RealTranslate = function(arg, arg2)
    if arg == "" then
        return ""
    end
    local str = "https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=auto&tl=" .. arg2 .. "&dt=t&q=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(arg)

    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(str)
    end)

    if ok then
        local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(result)
        if data and data[1] and data[1][1] then
            return data[1][1][1]
        end
    end

    return arg
end

SendChat = function(arg)
    if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
        local rbxGeneral = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
        if rbxGeneral then
            rbxGeneral:SendAsync(arg)
            return true
        end
    end

    local sayMessageRequest = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest", true)
    if not sayMessageRequest then
        return false
    end
    sayMessageRequest:FireServer(arg, "All")
    return true
end

IsJapanese = function(arg)
    local pos = arg:find("[぀-゙]")
    local pos2 = pos or arg:find("[ァ-ヶ]") or arg:find("[一-龅]")
    return pos2
end

OnMessageReceived = function(arg, arg2)
    if not autoTranslateEnabled or not arg or arg == Players.LocalPlayer then
        return
    end

    if IsJapanese(arg2) then
        return
    end
    local str = arg.LocaleId:lower()
    local v3 = LocaleToCountry[str]
    local str2 = v3 or "🏳️ (" .. str .. ")"
    local v4 = RealTranslate(arg2, "ja")
    if v4:lower() == arg2:lower() then
        return
    end

    if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
        local rbxGeneral = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")

        if rbxGeneral then
            rbxGeneral:DisplaySystemMessage("<font color='#00FF7F'>[" .. arg.DisplayName .. " " .. str2 .. " 訳]: " .. v4 .. "</font>")
        end
    else
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", {
            Text = "[" .. arg.DisplayName .. " " .. str2 .. " 訳]: " .. v4,
            Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 127),
            Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
        })
    end
end

TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(function(arg)
    if arg.TextSource then
        local playerByUserId = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(arg.TextSource.UserId)
        OnMessageReceived(playerByUserId, arg.Text)
    end
end)

hookedPlayers = {}

hookLegacyChat = function(arg)
    if hookedPlayers[arg] then
        return
    end
    hookedPlayers[arg] = true

    arg.Chatted:Connect(function(message)
        OnMessageReceived(arg, message)
    end)
end

for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    hookLegacyChat(player)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookLegacyChat)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    hookedPlayers[player] = nil
end)

notify = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v3 = Rayfield
    local rayfield = v3 or _G.Rayfield

    if rayfield and rayfield.Notify then
        local notify_ = rayfield.Notify
        local tbl3 = {}
        local title = arg or "Notification"
        tbl3.Title = title
        local content = arg2 or ""
        tbl3.Content = content
        local duration = arg3 or 3
        tbl3.Duration = duration
        notify_(rayfield, tbl3)
    else
        print("[" .. tostring(arg) .. "] " .. tostring(arg2))
    end
end

anchoredParts = {}
anchoredConnections = {}
anchorGrabEnabled = false
onPartOwnerAdded = nil
toggleBringEnabled = true
R_raaa = 10

isDescendantOf = function(arg, arg2)
    local parent = arg.Parent

    while parent do
        if parent == arg2 then
            return true
        end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end

    return false
end

getNearestPlayer = function()
    local v3 = nil
    local huge = math.huge
    local character_ = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_ or not character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        return nil
    end

    for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local magnitude = (character_.HumanoidRootPart.Position - player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

            if magnitude < huge then
                huge = magnitude
                v3 = player
            end
        end
    end

    return v3
end

cleanupConnections = function(arg)
    for i = #arg, 1, -1 do
        arg[i]:Disconnect()
        table.remove(arg, i)
    end
end

createHighlight = function(parent)
    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
    highlight.FillTransparency = 1
    highlight.Name = "Highlight"
    highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 1)
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.5
    highlight.Parent = parent
    return highlight
end

createBodyMovers = function(parent, position, cFrame)
    local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
    local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bodyPosition.P = 15000
    bodyPosition.D = 200
    bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(5000000, 5000000, 5000000)
    bodyPosition.Position = position
    bodyPosition.Parent = parent
    bodyGyro.P = 15000
    bodyGyro.D = 200
    bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(5000000, 5000000, 5000000)
    bodyGyro.CFrame = cFrame
    bodyGyro.Parent = parent
end

cleanupAnchoredParts = function()
    for i, v3 in ipairs(anchoredParts) do
        if v3 then
            if v3:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then
                v3.BodyPosition:Destroy()
            end

            if v3:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then
                v3.BodyGyro:Destroy()
            end

            if v3.Parent and v3.Parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
                v3.Parent.Highlight:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    cleanupConnections(anchoredConnections)
    anchoredParts = {}
end

anchorGrab = function()
    while anchorGrabEnabled do
        pcall(function()
            local grabParts = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
            if not grabParts then
                return
            end
            local grabPart = grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if not grabPart then
                return
            end
            local weldConstraint = grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
            if not weldConstraint or not weldConstraint.Part1 then
                return
            end
            local parent = weldConstraint.Part1.Parent
            if not parent then
                return
            end
            local primaryPart = parent.PrimaryPart
            if not primaryPart then
                return
            end

            if isDescendantOf(primaryPart, Workspace.Map) then
                return
            end

            for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player.Character and isDescendantOf(primaryPart, player.Character) then
                    return
                end
            end

            if not table.find(anchoredParts, primaryPart) then
                createHighlight(primaryPart.Parent)
                table.insert(anchoredParts, primaryPart)

                local connection2 = primaryPart.Parent.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                    if onPartOwnerAdded then
                        onPartOwnerAdded(descendant, primaryPart)
                    end
                end)

                table.insert(anchoredConnections, connection2)
            end

            for i, child in ipairs(primaryPart:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BodyMover") or child:IsA("BodyPosition") or child:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end

            while Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") and anchorGrabEnabled do
                task.wait()
            end

            if anchorGrabEnabled then
                createBodyMovers(primaryPart, primaryPart.Position, primaryPart.CFrame)
            end
        end)

        task.wait()
    end
end

processPart = function(parent)
    local character_ = localPlayer.Character
    if not toggleBringEnabled or not character_ or not character_:FindFirstChild("Head") or not parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        return
    end

    if parent.Anchored or parent:IsDescendantOf(character_) then
        return
    end

    if parent.Name == "Torso" or parent.Name == "Head" or parent.Name == "Right Arm" or parent.Name == "Left Arm" or parent.Name == "Right Leg" or parent.Name == "Left Leg" or parent.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
        return
    end

    if not parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyPosition") then
        local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(0, R_raaa, 0)
        bodyPosition.P = 6000000000000
        bodyPosition.D = 6000000
        bodyPosition.Parent = parent
    end
end

Type1 = {
    systemOn = false,
    autoRespawnEnabled = false,
    currentSeat = nil,
    ragdollConnection = nil,
    sitConnection = nil,
    characterAddedConnection = nil,
    Remotes = {ragdoll = nil, spawn = nil, destroy = nil},
}

Type1_CacheRemotes = function()
    if not Type1.Remotes.ragdoll then
        local characterEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("CharacterEvents", 5)

        if characterEvents then
            Type1.Remotes.ragdoll = characterEvents:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote")
        end
    end

    if not Type1.Remotes.spawn or not Type1.Remotes.destroy then
        local menuToys = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys", 5)

        if menuToys then
            Type1.Remotes.spawn = menuToys:WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction", 5)
            Type1.Remotes.destroy = menuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
        end
    end
end

Type1_StartRagdollSpam = function(arg)
    if Type1.ragdollConnection then
        Type1.ragdollConnection:Disconnect()
    end

    if not Type1.Remotes.ragdoll then
        return
    end

    Type1.ragdollConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Type1.systemOn or not arg then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            Type1.Remotes.ragdoll:FireServer(arg, 2)
        end)
    end)
end

Type1_StartSitSpam = function(arg, arg2)
    if Type1.sitConnection then
        Type1.sitConnection:Disconnect()
    end

    Type1.sitConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Type1.systemOn or not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
            return
        end
        arg2:Sit(arg)
    end)
end

Type1_CleanUpOldToys = function()
    if not Type1.Remotes.destroy then
        return
    end
    local v3 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    if not v3 then
        return
    end

    for k, child in pairs(v3:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            pcall(function()
                Type1.Remotes.destroy:FireServer(child)
            end)
        end
    end
end

Type1_RunStableGucci = function()
    if Type1.systemOn then
        return
    end
    local character_ = localPlayer.Character
    local result = character_ or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoid_ = result:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = result:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)

    if humanoid_ and humanoidRootPart_ then
        Type1.systemOn = true
        Type1_CleanUpOldToys()
        local cFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
        local cframe = CFrame.new(103.85, -7.45, -538.58)

        task.spawn(function()
            if Type1.Remotes.spawn then
                Type1.Remotes.spawn:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", cframe, Vector3.zero)
            end
        end)

        local v3 = nil
        local v4 = Workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 5)
        if not v4 then
            Type1.systemOn = false
            return
        end
        local now = tick()

        while now < 3 do
            local creatureBlobman = v4:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
            if not creatureBlobman then
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                continue
            end
            v3 = creatureBlobman
            break
        end

        if not v3 then
            Type1.systemOn = false
            return
        end
        local head = v3:FindFirstChild("Head")

        if head then
            head.Anchored = true
        end

        local vehicleSeat = v3:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 2)
        if not vehicleSeat then
            Type1.systemOn = false
            return
        end
        Type1.currentSeat = vehicleSeat
        Type1_StartRagdollSpam(humanoidRootPart_)
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = vehicleSeat.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
        vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid_)
        Type1_StartSitSpam(humanoid_, vehicleSeat)
        task.wait(0.6)
        humanoid_.Sit = false
        humanoid_.PlatformStand = true

        if vehicleSeat then
            vehicleSeat.Disabled = true

            pcall(function()
                vehicleSeat:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        if v3 then
            for k, descendant in pairs(v3:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("Snap") then
                    descendant:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end

        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame
        humanoidRootPart_.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        humanoid_.PlatformStand = false
        task.wait(0.5)

        if Type1.sitConnection then
            Type1.sitConnection:Disconnect()
            Type1.sitConnection = nil
        end

        if Type1.ragdollConnection then
            Type1.ragdollConnection:Disconnect()
            Type1.ragdollConnection = nil
        end

        task.wait(0.5)
        Type1.systemOn = false
        notify("Anti Gucci", "Anti Gucci (Blobman) Execution Completed!", 3)
        return
    end

    Type1.systemOn = false
end

Type1_StopSystem = function()
    Type1.systemOn = false
    Type1.autoRespawnEnabled = false
    Type1.currentSeat = nil

    if Type1.ragdollConnection then
        Type1.ragdollConnection:Disconnect()
        Type1.ragdollConnection = nil
    end

    if Type1.sitConnection then
        Type1.sitConnection:Disconnect()
        Type1.sitConnection = nil
    end
end

Type1_ConnectDeathEvent = function(arg)
    local humanoid_ = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not humanoid_ then
        return
    end

    humanoid_.Died:Connect(function()
        if Type1.autoRespawnEnabled then
            Type1.systemOn = false
            Type1_CleanUpOldToys()
        end
    end)
end

local function fn11()
    if thread then
        return
    end
    flag4 = true

    thread = task.spawn(function()
        local now = tick()

        while flag4 do

            if character_2 then
                local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart_ and ragdollRemote then
                    ragdollRemote:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 1)
                end

                if not flag5 and tick() - now >= 0.1 then
                    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    if humanoid_2 then
                        humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                        flag5 = true
                    end
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

local function fn14()
    local v3 = fn13()
    if v3 and v3:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman") then
        return true
    end

    local spawnToyRemoteFunction = menuToys and menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")

    if spawnToyRemoteFunction and pcall(function()
        spawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", CFrame.new(0, 100000, 0), Vector3.zero)
    end) then
        task.wait(0.1)
        local creatureBlobman = v3 and v3:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")

        if creatureBlobman then

                for k, descendant in pairs(creatureBlobman:GetDescendants()) do

                end

            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

                end
            end
        end

        task.wait()
    end
end

    local v3 = fn13()

        end
    end

    if not vehicleSeat then

    end
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character

        return

    if thread2 then

    thread2 = task.spawn(fn17)
    task.wait(0.5)
    fn16()
end

createNameLabel = function(parent, arg)
    if parent:FindFirstChild("__esp_namelabel") then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end
    local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboardGui.Name = "__esp_namelabel"
    billboardGui.Adornee = humanoidRootPart_
    billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
    billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboardGui.Parent = parent
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -70, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Parent = billboardGui
    local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    pcall(function()
        imageLabel.Image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(arg.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150)
    end)

    imageLabel.Parent = frame
    local uiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    uiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    uiCorner.Parent = imageLabel
    local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 40)
    textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -30, 0, 0)
    textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    textLabel.Text = arg.DisplayName .. " (@" .. arg.Name .. ")"
    textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    textLabel.TextScaled = true
    textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    textLabel.Parent = billboardGui
    table.insert(NameLabels, billboardGui)
end

enableNameLabels = function()
    for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
            createNameLabel(player.Character, player)
        end
    end
end

disableNameLabels = function()
    for i, v3 in ipairs(NameLabels) do
        pcall(function()
            v3:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    NameLabels = {}
end

setupPlayer = function(arg)
    if arg == localPlayer then
        return
    end

    local connection2 = arg.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character_2)
        task.wait(0.5)

        if ShowPlayerInfo then
            createNameLabel(character_2, arg)
        end
    end)

    PlayerConnections[arg] = connection2

    if arg.Character and ShowPlayerInfo then
        createNameLabel(arg.Character, arg)
    end
end

removePlayer = function(arg)
    if PlayerConnections[arg] then
        PlayerConnections[arg]:Disconnect()
        PlayerConnections[arg] = nil
    end
end

for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    setupPlayer(player)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local n, n2, v3, v4, v5, v6, v7, v8, v9, n3
    n3 = 1

    while true do
        if n3 == 1 then
            task.wait(1)

            if ShowPlayerInfo then
                n3 = 2
            else
                n3 = 1
            end
        elseif n3 == 2 then
            n = #NameLabels - -1
            n3 = 3
        elseif n3 == 3 then
            n2 = n + -1

            if n2 >= 1 then
                n3 = 8
            else
                n3 = 4
            end
        elseif n3 == 4 then
            v3, v4, v5 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
            v6 = v5
            n3 = 5
        elseif n3 == 5 then
            v7, v8 = v3(v4, v6)

            if v7 == nil then
                n3 = 1
            else
                n3 = 6
            end
        elseif n3 == 6 then
            v6 = v7

            if v8 ~= localPlayer and v8.Character then
                n3 = 7
            else
                n3 = 5
            end
        elseif n3 == 7 then
            createNameLabel(v8.Character, v8)
            n3 = 5
        elseif n3 == 8 then
            n = n2
            v9 = NameLabels[n2]

            if v9 and v9.Parent then
                n3 = 3
            else
                n3 = 9
            end
        elseif n3 == 9 then
            table.remove(NameLabels, n2)
            n3 = 3
        end
    end
end)

refreshBlobman = function()
    local v5 = nil

    for i, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and descendant:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
            local vehicleSeat = descendant.VehicleSeat
            local seatWeld = vehicleSeat and vehicleSeat:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")
            if seatWeld and seatWeld.Part1 and seatWeld.Part1:IsDescendantOf(localPlayer.Character) then
                v5 = descendant
                break
            end
        end
    end

    cachedBlobman = v5

    if not v5 then
        cachedCG = nil
        cachedCD = nil
        cachedCR = nil
        cachedR_Det = nil
        cachedR_Weld = nil
        cachedL_Det = nil
        cachedL_Weld = nil
        return
    end

    local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = v5:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
    local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld = v5:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript[old]")
    local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab") or blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab") or v5:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab", true)
    cachedCG = creatureGrab
    local creatureDrop = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop") or blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop") or v5:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop", true)
    cachedCD = creatureDrop
    local creatureRelease = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease") or blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScriptOld:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease") or v5:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease", true)
    cachedCR = creatureRelease
    local rightDetector = v5:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
    cachedR_Det = rightDetector
    local v6 = cachedR_Det
    local weld = v6

    if v6 then
        local rightWeld = cachedR_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")
        weld = rightWeld or cachedR_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
    end

    cachedR_Weld = weld
    local leftDetector = v5:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
    cachedL_Det = leftDetector
    local v7 = cachedL_Det
    local weld2 = v7

    if v7 then
        local leftWeld = cachedL_Det:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
        weld2 = leftWeld or cachedL_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
    end

    cachedL_Weld = weld2
end

pcall(refreshBlobman)

Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
    if descendant.Name == "CreatureBlobman" or descendant.Name == "SeatWeld" then
        task.defer(refreshBlobman)
    end
end)

Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(descendant)
    if descendant == cachedBlobman or descendant.Name == "SeatWeld" then
        task.defer(refreshBlobman)
    end
end)

ensureSitting = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 then
        return false
    end

    if humanoid_2.SeatPart then
        return true
    end

    if cachedBlobman then
        local vehicleSeat = cachedBlobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

        if vehicleSeat then
            pcall(function()
                vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid_2)
            end)

            task.wait(0.1)
            local flag8 = humanoid_2.SeatPart == vehicleSeat
            return flag8
        end
    end

    return false
end

attackPlayer = function(arg)
    if not cachedBlobman then
        return
    end

    if not ensureSitting() then
        return
    end
    local character_2 = arg.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)

        if cachedCG then
            if cachedR_Det then
                cachedCG:FireServer(cachedR_Det, humanoidRootPart_, cachedR_Weld)
                local rightWeld = cachedR_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")
                local weld = rightWeld or cachedR_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                cachedR_Weld = weld

                if cachedCR and cachedR_Weld then
                    cachedCR:FireServer(cachedR_Weld)
                end

                if cachedCD and cachedR_Weld then
                    cachedCD:FireServer(cachedR_Weld)
                end
            end

            if cachedL_Det then
                cachedCG:FireServer(cachedL_Det, humanoidRootPart_, cachedL_Weld)
                local leftWeld = cachedL_Det:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                local weld = leftWeld or cachedL_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                cachedL_Weld = weld

                if cachedCR and cachedL_Weld then
                    cachedCR:FireServer(cachedL_Weld)
                end

                if cachedCD and cachedL_Weld then
                    cachedCD:FireServer(cachedL_Weld)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    task.wait(0.01)
end

bmkAuraEnabled = false
AURA_RADIUS = 35
auraThread = nil

getTargetsInRange = function()
    local tbl4 = {}
    local v5 = HRP()
    if not v5 then
        return tbl4
    end
    local position = v5.Position

    for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            local character_2 = player.Character
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and (position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude <= AURA_RADIUS then
                table.insert(tbl4, player)
            end
        end
    end

    return tbl4
end

stopBmkAura = function()
    bmkAuraEnabled = false
    auraThread = nil
end

startBmkAura = function()
    if auraThread then
        return
    end
    bmkAuraEnabled = true

    local thread3 = task.spawn(function()
        while bmkAuraEnabled do
            local v5 = getTargetsInRange()

            if #v5 > 0 then
                for i, v6 in ipairs(v5) do
                    if not bmkAuraEnabled then
                        break
                    end
                    attackPlayer(v6)
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.1)
        end

        auraThread = nil
    end)

    auraThread = thread3
end

    local tbl4 = {}

            table.insert(tbl4, player)
        end
    end

    return tbl4
end

GetMyBlobman = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return nil
    end
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 then
        return nil
    end
    local seatPart = humanoid_2.SeatPart
    if seatPart and seatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat") and seatPart.Parent and seatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
        return seatPart.Parent
    end

    if cachedBlobman then
        local vehicleSeat = cachedBlobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

        if vehicleSeat then
            local seatWeld = vehicleSeat:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")
            if seatWeld and seatWeld.Part1 and seatWeld.Part1:IsDescendantOf(character_2) then
                return cachedBlobman
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

bmkNoclipConnection = nil
bmkBodyVelocityChar = nil
bmkBodyVelocitySeat = nil
bmkBodyGyroSeat = nil

startBmkNoclip = function()
    if bmkNoclipConnection then
        return
    end

    local connection2 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
        local v5 = GetMyBlobman()

        if character_2 then
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoidRootPart_ then
                if v5 then
                    if not bmkBodyVelocityChar or bmkBodyVelocityChar.Parent ~= humanoidRootPart_ then
                        if bmkBodyVelocityChar then
                            bmkBodyVelocityChar:Destroy()
                        end

                        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        bmkBodyVelocityChar = bodyVelocity
                        bmkBodyVelocityChar.Name = "AntiFall_Char"
                        bmkBodyVelocityChar.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        bmkBodyVelocityChar.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 99999, 0)
                        bmkBodyVelocityChar.Parent = humanoidRootPart_
                    end

                    for i, descendant in ipairs(character_2:GetDescendants()) do
                        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
                            descendant.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                elseif bmkBodyVelocityChar then
                    bmkBodyVelocityChar:Destroy()
                    bmkBodyVelocityChar = nil
                end
            end
        end

        if v5 then
            local vehicleSeat = v5:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

            if vehicleSeat then
                if not bmkBodyVelocitySeat or bmkBodyVelocitySeat.Parent ~= vehicleSeat then
                    if bmkBodyVelocitySeat then
                        bmkBodyVelocitySeat:Destroy()
                    end

                    local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    bmkBodyVelocitySeat = bodyVelocity
                    bmkBodyVelocitySeat.Name = "AntiFall_Seat"
                    bmkBodyVelocitySeat.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    bmkBodyVelocitySeat.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 99999, 0)
                    bmkBodyVelocitySeat.Parent = vehicleSeat
                end

                if not bmkBodyGyroSeat or bmkBodyGyroSeat.Parent ~= vehicleSeat then
                    if bmkBodyGyroSeat then
                        bmkBodyGyroSeat:Destroy()
                    end

                    local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat = bodyGyro
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat.Name = "Rotate_Seat"
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(400000, 400000, 400000)
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat.P = 10000
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat.Parent = vehicleSeat
                end

                if currentCamera then
                    local v6, v7 = currentCamera.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
                    bmkBodyGyroSeat.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, v7, 0)
                end
            end

            for i, descendant in ipairs(v5:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
                    descendant.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        else
            if bmkBodyVelocitySeat then
                bmkBodyVelocitySeat:Destroy()
                bmkBodyVelocitySeat = nil
            end

            if bmkBodyGyroSeat then
                bmkBodyGyroSeat:Destroy()
                bmkBodyGyroSeat = nil
            end
        end
    end)

    bmkNoclipConnection = connection2
end

stopBmkNoclip = function()
    if bmkNoclipConnection then
        bmkNoclipConnection:Disconnect()
        bmkNoclipConnection = nil
    end

    if bmkBodyVelocityChar then
        bmkBodyVelocityChar:Destroy()
        bmkBodyVelocityChar = nil
    end

    if bmkBodyVelocitySeat then
        bmkBodyVelocitySeat:Destroy()
        bmkBodyVelocitySeat = nil
    end

    if bmkBodyGyroSeat then
        bmkBodyGyroSeat:Destroy()
        bmkBodyGyroSeat = nil
    end
end

ApplyAntiRagdoll = function(arg)
    if not arg or not AntiRagBlob then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not humanoid_2 or not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if antiRagdollCons.ARSeat then
        antiRagdollCons.ARSeat:Disconnect()
    end

    antiRagdollCons.ARSeat = humanoid_2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SeatPart"):Connect(function()
        if humanoid_2.SeatPart and humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent and humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and not RagdolledSit then
            RagdolledSit = true
            local seatPart = humanoid_2.SeatPart

            while not humanoid_2.Sit do
                task.wait()
            end

            pcall(function()
                ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 3)
            end)

            local ragdolled = humanoid_2:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

            while ragdolled and not ragdolled.Value and not humanoid_2.Sit do
                task.wait()
            end

            task.wait(0.4)
            humanoid_2.Sit = false

            pcall(function()
                seatPart:Sit(humanoid_2)
            end)

            task.delay(0.25, function()
                while humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.SeatPart do
                    pcall(function()
                        ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 1)
                    end)

                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                RagdolledSit = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end

GrabItem = function(arg)
    if processing[arg] then
        return
    end
    processing[arg] = true
    local holdPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    if not holdPart then
        processing[arg] = nil
        return
    end
    local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
    local dropItemRemoteFunction = holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")

    if holdItemRemoteFunction and dropItemRemoteFunction then
        task.spawn(function()
            if pcall(function()
                holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg, GrabData.lp.Character)
            end) then
                pcall(function()
                    dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg, GrabData.dropPos, Vector3.new())
                end)
            end

            task.wait(2)
            processing[arg] = nil
        end)
    else
        processing[arg] = nil
    end
end

GrabFolder = function(arg)
    for i, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("Model") and descendant:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") and descendant.HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction") then
            GrabItem(descendant)
        end
    end
end

LoopGrab = function()
    while GrabData.toggle do
        for i, child in ipairs(GrabData.ws:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Folder") and child.Name:find("SpawnedInToys") then
                GrabFolder(child)
            end
        end

        local plotItems = GrabData.ws:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

        if plotItems then
            local v5, v6, v7 = pairs(plotItems:GetChildren())
            local v8 = v7
            local v9 = v5
            local v10 = v6

            while true do
                local v11, v12 = v9(v10, v8)
                if v11 == nil then
                    break
                end
                v8 = v11

                if v12.Name ~= "PlayersInPlots" then
                    for k, child in pairs(v12:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") and child.HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction") then
                            GrabItem(child)
                        end
                    end

                    v8 = v11
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end

    if not arg or not arg:IsA("Player") or not arg.Character then
        return false
    end
    local head = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local partOwner = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
    if not partOwner or partOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name then
        return false
    end

    if not arg2 then
        return true
    end
    return partOwner
end

        bodyVelocity.Name = "SkyVelocity"

        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    end
end

lookAt = function(arg, arg2)
    local unit = (arg2 - arg).Unit
    local v5 = unit:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    local v6 = v5:Cross(unit)
    return CFrame.fromMatrix(arg, v5, v6)
end

    if not arg or typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" then
        return
    end
    local v5 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg.Position)
    local v6 = getChar()

    if v6 and v5 <= 30 then
        setNetOwner:FireServer(arg, lookAt(v6.HumanoidRootPart.Position, arg.Position))
    end
end

claimPlayerOwner = function(arg, arg2)
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 or not arg or not arg.Character then
        return false
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end
    local v6 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(humanoidRootPart_.Position)

    if not checkPlayerOwner(arg) then
        if v6 <= 30 then
            setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, lookAt(v5.HumanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart_.Position))
        end

        return
    end

    if type(arg2) == "function" then
        arg2()
    end

    return true
end

getHomePlot = function(arg)
    if not arg then
        return false
    end
    local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
    if not plotItems then
        return false
    end
    local playersInPlots = plotItems:FindFirstChild("PlayersInPlots")
    if not playersInPlots or not playersInPlots:FindFirstChild(arg.Name) then
        return false
    end
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        return false
    end
    local v5, v6, v7 = ipairs(plots:GetChildren())
    local v8 = v7
    local v9 = v5
    local v10 = v6

    while true do
        local v11, v12 = v9(v10, v8)
        if v11 == nil then
            break
        end
        v8 = v11
        local plotSign = v12:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
        if not thisPlotsOwners then
            continue
        end

        for i, child in ipairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Value == arg.Name then
                local v13 = plotItems:FindFirstChild(v12.Name)
                if v13 then
                    return true, v13
                end
            end
        end

        v8 = v11
    end

    return false
end

getGroupRank = function(arg, arg2)
    if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg.Parent then
        local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("LastTimeRankUpdate")

        if not attribute or os.clock() - attribute >= 300 then
            local ok, result2 = pcall(function()
                return arg:GetRankInGroup(arg2)
            end)

            local ok2, result3 = pcall(function()
                return arg:GetRoleInGroup(arg2)
            end)

            local flag8

            if ok then
                flag8 = false
            else
                flag8 = true
            end

            if flag8 then
                flag8 = "Common"
            end

            if not flag8 then
                flag8 = result2
            end

            if flag8 == 255 then
                arg:SetAttribute("Rank", "Leader")
            elseif flag8 == 4 then
                if result3 == "High Rank Admin" then
                    arg:SetAttribute("Rank", "High Rank Admin")
                end
            elseif flag8 == 3 then
                arg:SetAttribute("Rank", "Low Rank Admin")
            elseif flag8 == 2 then
                arg:SetAttribute("Rank", "Goon")
            elseif flag8 == 0 or flag8 == 1 then
                arg:SetAttribute("Rank", "Common")
            end

            arg:SetAttribute("LastTimeRankUpdate", os.clock())
        end
    end

    local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("Rank")
    local str = attribute or "Common"
    return str
end

isAuthorized = function(arg)
    local playerFromCharacter = arg

    if typeof(arg) == "Instance" then
        if arg:IsA("Model") and arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg) then
            playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg)
        elseif not arg:IsA("Player") then
            return false
        end
    else
        playerFromCharacter = nil
    end

    if not playerFromCharacter then
        return false
    end
    local v5 = getGroupRank(playerFromCharacter, 16168861)
    local flag8 = v5 == "Leader" or v5 == "High Rank Admin" or v5 == "Low Rank Admin" or v5 == "Goon"
    local v6 = flag8
    return v6
end

isPlayerWhitelisted = function(arg)
    local flag8 = table.find(processedInstances, arg) ~= nil
    return flag8
end

    if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg ~= localPlayer and not isAuthorized(arg) and arg.Character and arg.Character:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) and arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and arg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and arg.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

end

    end
    return false

SNOWshipPlayerKick = function(arg)
    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg:IsA("Player") and arg.Character then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart_ then
            local v5 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(humanoidRootPart_.Position)
            if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(arg) then
                return true
            end

            if v5 <= 30 then
                setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart_.Position))
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer = function(arg, arg2)
    if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Player") or not arg.Character or not arg.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or not arg.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or arg.Character.Head.PartOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name then
        return
    end
    local flag8

    if arg2 then
        flag8 = false
    else
        flag8 = true
    end

    if flag8 then
        flag8 = true
    end

    if not flag8 then
        flag8 = arg.Character.Head.PartOwner
    end

    return flag8
end

local function fn21(arg)
    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and CheckPlayer(arg) then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart_ then
            local v5 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(humanoidRootPart_.Position)
            if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(arg) then
                return true
            end

            if v5 <= 25 then
                setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart_.Position))
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if _G.TPAura then
            for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if not _G.TPAura then
                    break
                end

                if CheckPlayer(player) then
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart_ and fn21(player) then
                        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

poisonPlayer = function(arg)
    if not arg or not arg.Character then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if arg.Character:GetAttribute("Poisoned") then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = arg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end

    if arg.Character:GetAttribute("PoisonTargeted") then
        return
    end
    arg.Character:SetAttribute("PoisonTargeted", true)

    task.spawn(function()
        local flag8 = false
        local v5 = HRP()

        if v5 and CheckPlayer(arg) then
            local humanoidRootPart_2 = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoidRootPart_2 then
                if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(arg) then
                    flag8 = true
                elseif (humanoidRootPart_2.Position - v5.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
                    setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_2, lookAt(v5.Position, humanoidRootPart_2.Position))
                    local now = os.clock()

                    while os.clock() - now < 0.25 do
                        if not CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(arg) then
                            task.wait()
                            continue
                        end
                        flag8 = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if flag8 then
            arg.Character:SetAttribute("Poisoned", true)

            pcall(function()
                local map2 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                local hole = map2 and map2:FindFirstChild("Hole")
                local poisonBigHole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole")
                local poisonHurtPart = poisonBigHole and poisonBigHole:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")

                if poisonHurtPart then
                    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = poisonHurtPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                end
            end)

            task.delay(4, function()
                if arg and arg.Character then
                    arg.Character:SetAttribute("Poisoned", nil)
                    arg.Character:SetAttribute("PoisonTargeted", nil)
                end
            end)
        elseif arg and arg.Character then
            arg.Character:SetAttribute("PoisonTargeted", nil)
        end
    end)
end

startPoisonAura = function()
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.PoisonAuraEnabled do
            local v5 = HRP()

            if v5 then
                for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if not _G.PoisonAuraEnabled then
                        break
                    end

                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                        local humanoidRootPart_ = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if humanoidRootPart_ and (humanoidRootPart_.Position - v5.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
                            poisonPlayer(player)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

SNOWshipPlayer = function(arg, arg2)
    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg:IsA("Player") and arg.Character and arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local v5 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(humanoidRootPart_.Position)

        if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(arg) then
            if type(arg2) == "function" then
                arg2()
            end

            return true
        end

        if v5 <= 30 then
            setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart_.Position))
        end
    end

    return false
end

    return false
end

CheckPlayerAurasLock = function(arg)
    if CheckPlayer(arg) and (not fn22(arg.Name) or not _G.WhitelistFriends) and not fn22(arg.Name) and not arg.Character:GetAttribute("Kicking") and not _G.KickAura then

    end

end

CreateSkyVelocity = function(arg)
    if not arg:FindFirstChild("SkyVelocity") then
        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", arg)
        bodyVelocity.Name = "SkyVelocity"
        bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 100000000000000, 0)
        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    end
end

local function fn23(arg)
    return math.floor(arg + 0.5)
end

GetPlayerCharacter = function()
    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        return localPlayer.Character
    end
    return nil
end

TeleportPlayer = function(arg, arg2)
    local n = arg2 or 0
    local v5 = n

    if v5 == _G.TP_Priority then
        local v6 = GetPlayerCharacter()

        if v6 and not _G.TeleportingToNetworkOwnership and typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
            local humanoidRootPart_ = v6.HumanoidRootPart
            local humanoid_2 = v6:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.Rotation + arg.Position

            if humanoid_2.SeatPart == nil or tostring(humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
                humanoid_2.Sit = false
            end
        end
    end
end

isPlayerSeatedInBlobman = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if character_2 and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Sit and humanoid_2.SeatPart ~= nil and tostring(humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent) == "CreatureBlobman" then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

IsPlayerKickingWithBlobman = function()
    if isPlayerSeatedInBlobman() and _G.LoopKick then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

teleportplayerfunctionoffset = function(arg)
    local cframe = nil
    local n = TeleportPlayerOffset + 1

    if _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Behind" then
        cframe = CFrame.new(arg.Position - arg.LookVector * n)
    elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Front" then
        cframe = CFrame.new(arg.Position + arg.LookVector * n)
    elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Right" then
        cframe = CFrame.new(arg.Position + arg.RightVector * n)
    elseif _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection == "Left" then
        cframe = CFrame.new(arg.Position - arg.RightVector * n)
    end

    if cframe then
        TeleportPlayer(cframe)
    end
end

getTPDropdownList = function()
    local tbl4 = {}

    for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            table.insert(tbl4, player.Name .. " (" .. player.DisplayName .. ")")
        end
    end

    table.sort(tbl4, function(arg, arg2)
        local flag8 = arg:lower() < arg2:lower()
        return flag8
    end)

    return tbl4
end

FWC = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v5 = arg:FindFirstChild(arg2)
    local v6 = v5 or arg:WaitForChild(arg2, arg3)
    return v6
end

end

                    n = 3
                else

                end

                    n = 4

            return notify("Bring Error", "ターゲットを選択してください。", 4)

end

local function fn24(arg)
    if timer ~= 0 then
        return
    end

    if arg == "" then
        return notify("Switch Error", "ターゲットを選択してください。", 4)
    end
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    if not v5 then
        return notify("Switch error", "Player left", 4)
    end
    local character_2 = v5.Character
    local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local head = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not character_2 or not humanoid_2 or not humanoidRootPart_ or not head then
        return notify("Switch Error", "ターゲットの部位が見つかりません。", 4)
    end

    if humanoid_2.Health == 0 or v5:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and v5.InPlot.Value then
        return notify("Switch Error", "Player died or in safe-zone", 4)
    end
    local character_3 = localPlayer.Character
    local result2 = character_3 or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local v6 = result2 and FWC(result2, "HumanoidRootPart")
    local v7 = result2 and FWC(result2, "Humanoid")
    if not result2 or not v6 or not v7 then
        return
    end
    local cFrame2 = v6.CFrame

    while task.wait() and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.Parent and v7.Health ~= 0 and (not head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value ~= localPlayer.Name) do
        if timer > 75 then
            v6.CFrame = cFrame2
            timer = 0
            return
        end

        timer = timer + 1
        local isHeld_ = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("IsHeld")

        if not Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(arg) and (not isHeld_ or isHeld_.Value ~= true) and not WaterBring then
            v6.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
            task.defer(grab, humanoidRootPart_)
            continue
        end

        v6.CFrame = cFrame2
        return
    end

    for k, child in pairs(character_2:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Part") then
            child.Velocity = Vector3.new()
        end
    end

    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2

    if GrabFlag == "Default" then
        Destroy_Line(humanoidRootPart_)
    end

    for k, child in pairs(result2:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Part") then
            child.Velocity = Vector3.new()
        end
    end

    timer = 0
end

TBot = {
    Enabled = false,
    Connection = nil,
    canGrab = true,
    maxDistance = 20,
    preGrabDelay = 1e-05,
    postGrabDelay = 0.05,
    lastTarget = nil,
    lastHitTime = 0,
    targetMemoryDuration = 0.1,
    checkThrottle = 0.008,
    lastCheck = 0,
}

local raycastParams_ = RaycastParams.new()
raycastParams = raycastParams_
raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

task.spawn(function()
    local ok, result2 = pcall(function()
        return ReplicatedStorage.GamepassEvents.CheckForGamepass:InvokeServer(20837132)
    end)

    if ok and result2 then
        TBot.maxDistance = 29.3
    end
end)

if ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GamepassEvents") and ReplicatedStorage.GamepassEvents:FindFirstChild("FurtherReachBoughtNotifier") then
    ReplicatedStorage.GamepassEvents.FurtherReachBoughtNotifier.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
        TBot.maxDistance = 29.3
    end)
end

TBot.GetTarget = function(arg)
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end

    if Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") then
        return
    end
    local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local position = currentCamera2.CFrame.Position
    local lookVector = currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector
    raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {v5, Workspace.Terrain}
    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(position, lookVector * 1000, raycastParams)
    local v6 = hit

    if not hit then
        local tbl4 = {
            lookVector,
            (lookVector + Vector3.new(0, 0.075, 0)).Unit,
            (lookVector - Vector3.new(0, 0.075, 0)).Unit,
        }

        local v7, v8, v9 = ipairs(tbl4)
        local v10 = v9
        local v11, v12, hit2

        repeat
            v11, v12 = v7(v8, v10)
            if v11 == nil then
                break
            end
            v10 = v11
            hit2 = Workspace:Raycast(position, v12 * 1000, raycastParams)
            v6 = hit2
        until hit2
    end

    if not v6 then
        return
    end
    local model = v6.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not model or not model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or model == v5 then
        return
    end

    if model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if arg.maxDistance < (v5.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude then
        return
    end
    return model
end

TBot.OnHeartbeat = function(arg)
    if not arg.Enabled or not arg.canGrab then
        return
    end

    if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
        return
    end

    if tick() - arg.lastCheck < arg.checkThrottle then
        return
    end
    arg.lastCheck = tick()
    local target = arg:GetTarget()

    if target then
        arg.lastTarget = target
        arg.lastHitTime = tick()
    elseif arg.lastTarget then
        local n = tick() - arg.lastHitTime

        if arg.targetMemoryDuration < n then
            arg.lastTarget = nil
        end
    end

    local v5 = getChar()
    local lastTarget = arg.lastTarget
    local humanoidRootPart_ = lastTarget and arg.lastTarget:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not arg.lastTarget or not v5 or not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if arg.maxDistance < (v5.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude then
        arg.lastTarget = nil
        return
    end

    if arg.lastTarget then
        arg.canGrab = false

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(arg.preGrabDelay)
            pcall(mouse1press)
            local now = tick()

            repeat
                task.wait(0.02)
            until not Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") or tick() - now > 1.6

            task.wait(arg.postGrabDelay)
            arg.canGrab = true
            arg.lastTarget = nil
        end)
    end
end

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            local grabPart = child:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            local weldConstraint = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
            local part1 = weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1
            if not part1 then
                return
            end
            local bodyVelocity = nil

            if _G.SuperStrength then
                bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new()
                bodyVelocity.Name = "SuperStrength"
                bodyVelocity.Parent = part1
            end

            if bodyVelocity then
                task.spawn(function()
                    if not localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ContextActionGui") then
                        return
                    end
                    local parent = nil
                    local connection2 = nil

                    while parent == nil and child.Parent do
                        for i, descendant in ipairs(localPlayer.PlayerGui.ContextActionGui:GetDescendants()) do
                            if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") and descendant.Image == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9603678090" then
                                parent = descendant.Parent
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end

                    if parent then
                        parent.Active = true

                        connection2 = parent.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                            if _G.SuperStrength and bodyVelocity.Parent then
                                local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = vector3
                                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * _G.Strength
                            end
                        end)
                    end

                    local connection3 = nil

                    connection3 = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
                        if not child.Parent then
                            Debris:AddItem(bodyVelocity, 1)

                            if connection2 then
                                connection2:Disconnect()
                            end

                            connection3:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end)
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                    local connection2 = nil

                    connection2 = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
                        if not child.Parent then
                            if UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and _G.SuperStrength then
                                local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = vector3
                                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * _G.Strength
                                Debris:AddItem(bodyVelocity, 1)
                            elseif UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and bodyVelocity.Parent then
                                bodyVelocity:Destroy()
                            end

                            connection2:Disconnect()
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            if not _G.Death_Grab then
                return
            end
            local grabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 5)
            local weldConstraint = grabPart and grabPart:WaitForChild("WeldConstraint", 5)
            local part1 = weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1

            if part1 and part1.Parent then
                local parent = part1.Parent
                local humanoid_2 = parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if humanoid_2 then
                    while child.Parent and _G.Death_Grab do
                        local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(parent)

                        if playerFromCharacter and checkPlayerOwner(playerFromCharacter) then
                            humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            humanoid_2.Jump = true
                            humanoid_2.Sit = false

                            if humanoid_2:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then
                                destroyLine:FireServer(part1)
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            if not SpinGrabFTAPEnabled then
                return
            end
            local grabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 5)

            if grabPart then
                pcall(function()
                    local weldConstraint = grabPart:WaitForChild("WeldConstraint", 5)

                    if weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1 then
                        local bodyAngularVelocity = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity", weldConstraint.Part1)
                        bodyAngularVelocity.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
                        bodyAngularVelocity.MaxTorque = Vector3.one * math.huge
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            if not _G.NoclipGrab then
                return
            end
            local grabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 5)
            local weldConstraint = grabPart and grabPart:WaitForChild("WeldConstraint", 5)
            local part1 = weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1

            while not part1 and child.Parent do
                task.wait()
                local part12 = weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1
                part1 = part12
            end

            if part1 and not part1.Anchored then
                local parent = part1.Parent

                if parent and parent:IsA("Model") then
                    local descendants = parent:GetDescendants()
                    local humanoid_2 = parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local tbl4 = {}

                    for i, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do
                        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                            tbl4[descendant] = descendant.CanCollide
                        end
                    end

                    while child.Parent do
                        for i, descendant in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
                            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                descendant.CanCollide = false
                            end
                        end

                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end

                    if humanoid_2 then
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end

                    for k, v5 in pairs(tbl4) do
                        if k and k.Parent then
                            k.CanCollide = v5
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            if not _G.MassLessGrab then
                return
            end
            local dragPart = child:WaitForChild("DragPart", 5)

            if dragPart then
                local alignOrientation = dragPart:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation")
                local alignPosition = dragPart:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")

                if alignOrientation then
                    alignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
                    alignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
                end

                if alignPosition then
                    alignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
                    alignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            local dragPart = child:WaitForChild("DragPart", 5)

            if dragPart then
                local dragAttach = dragPart:WaitForChild("DragAttach", 5)

                if dragAttach then
                    while child.Parent and task.wait() do
                        if grabZoomEnabled then
                            dragAttach.Position = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * grabZoomValue
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

changePriority = function(tpPriority)
    if _G.TP_Priority <= tpPriority then
        _G.TP_Priority = tpPriority
        return true
    end

    if tpPriority ~= 0 then
        return false
    end
    _G.TP_Priority = tpPriority
    return true
end

tpTo = function(arg, arg2)
    local n = arg2 or 0
    local v5 = n

    if v5 == _G.TP_Priority then
        local v6 = getChar()

        if v6 and not _G.TeleportingToNetworkOwnership and typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
            local humanoidRootPart_ = v6.HumanoidRootPart
            local humanoid_2 = v6:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.Rotation + arg.Position

            if humanoid_2.SeatPart == nil or tostring(humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
                humanoid_2.Sit = false
            end
        end
    end
end

tpSimple = function(arg)
    local v5 = getChar()

    if v5 and typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = v5.HumanoidRootPart
        local humanoid_2 = v5:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.Rotation + arg.Position
        humanoid_2.Sit = false
    end
end

floatConn = nil
isFloatingState = nil

startFloat = function()
    if not floatConn then
        isFloatingState = false

        local connection2 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            local v5 = getChar()

            if isFloatingState == false and v5 then
