                for i, child in ipairs(v5:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide then
                        child.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        floatConn = connection2
    end
end

startAntiGucciTrain = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid_2 or not humanoidRootPart_ or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    safePositionTrain = humanoidRootPart_.Position
    local map2 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local alwaysHereTweenedObjects = map2 and Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
    local train = alwaysHereTweenedObjects and alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Train")
    local v5 = nil

    if train then
        for i, descendant in ipairs(train:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("Seat") then
                v5 = descendant
                break
            end
            continue
        end
    end

    if v5 then
        pcall(function()
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = v5.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            v5:Sit(humanoid_2)
        end)
    end

    if jumpConnection then
        pcall(function()
            jumpConnection:Disconnect()
        end)

        jumpConnection = nil
    end

    pcall(function()
        local connection2 = humanoid_2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Jump"):Connect(function()
            if humanoid_2.Jump and humanoid_2.Sit then
                restoreFramesTrain = 15
                safePositionTrain = humanoidRootPart_.Position
            end
        end)

        jumpConnection = connection2
    end)

    if antiGucciConnectionTrain then
        pcall(function()
            antiGucciConnectionTrain:Disconnect()
        end)

        antiGucciConnectionTrain = nil
    end

    pcall(function()
        local connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
                return
            end
            local characterEvents2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CharacterEvents")
            local ragdollRemote2 = characterEvents2 and characterEvents2:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote")

            if ragdollRemote2 then
                pcall(function()
                    ragdollRemote2:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 0)
                end)
            end

            if restoreFramesTrain > 0 then
                humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(safePositionTrain)
                restoreFramesTrain = restoreFramesTrain - 1
            end
        end)

        antiGucciConnectionTrain = connection2
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Sit and humanoid_2.Health > 0 do
            task.wait(1)
        end

        task.wait(0.5)

        if autoGucciActiveTrain and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(safePositionTrain)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

stopFloat = function()
    if floatConn then
        floatConn:Disconnect()
        floatConn = nil
    end

    isFloatingState = true
end

startFloating = function()
    if not floatSteppedConnection then
        isFloating = false

        local connection2 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if isFloating == false and localPlayer.Character ~= nil then
                for k, child in pairs(localPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.CanCollide then
                        child.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        floatSteppedConnection = connection2
    end
end

toggleNoclip = function()
    if floatSteppedConnection then
        floatSteppedConnection:Disconnect()
        floatSteppedConnection = nil
    end

    isFloating = true
end

StopAntiGrab = function()
    AntiGrabEnabled = false

    if HeldConnection then
        HeldConnection:Disconnect()
        HeldConnection = nil
    end

    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character

    if character_2 then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoidRootPart_ then
            humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        if humanoid_2 then
            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end

        for k, v5 in pairs(originalCanCollide) do
            if k and k.Parent then
                k.CanCollide = v5
            end
        end

        table.clear(originalCanCollide)
    end
end

StartAntiGrab = function()
    if HeldConnection then
        HeldConnection:Disconnect()
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local n = 0

        while not isHeld and n < 50 do
            task.wait(0.1)
            n = n + 1
        end

        if not isHeld then
            return
        end

        local connection2 = isHeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
            if not AntiGrabEnabled or not isHeld.Value then
                return
            end
            local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
            if not character_2 then
                return
            end
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            table.clear(originalCanCollide)

            for k, descendant in pairs(character_2:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                    originalCanCollide[descendant] = descendant.CanCollide
                    descendant.CanCollide = false
                end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while AntiGrabEnabled and isHeld.Value do
                    pcall(function()
                        if struggle then
                            struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                        end

                        local characterEvents2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CharacterEvents")

                        if characterEvents2 and characterEvents2:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote") then
                            characterEvents2.RagdollRemote:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 0)
                        end

                        local gameCorrectionEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionEvents")

                        if gameCorrectionEvents and gameCorrectionEvents:FindFirstChild("StopAllVelocity") then
                            gameCorrectionEvents.StopAllVelocity:FireServer()
                        end
                    end)

                    task.wait()
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while AntiGrabEnabled and isHeld.Value do
                    pcall(function()
                        if humanoid_2 then
                            humanoid_2.Sit = false
                            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                            humanoid_2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
                        end

                        if humanoidRootPart_ then
                            humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = true
                            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end)

                    task.wait()
                end

                if humanoidRootPart_ then
                    humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
                end
            end)
        end)

        HeldConnection = connection2

        if isHeld.Value and localPlayer.Character then
            local humanoidRootPart_ = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoidRootPart_ then
                humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = true
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while isHeld and isHeld.Value and AntiGrabEnabled do
                    if struggle then
                        struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                    end

                    task.wait()
                end

                if humanoidRootPart_ then
                    humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

enableFallenBypass = function()
    origFallenHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
    Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0 / 0)
end

disableFallenBypass = function()
    Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
end

lockCam = function()
    local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if currentCamera2 then
        if bypassCamPart then
            bypassCamPart:Destroy()
            bypassCamPart = nil
        end

        local part2 = Instance.new("Part")
        bypassCamPart = part2
        bypassCamPart.Name = "BypassCameraTarget"
        bypassCamPart.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        bypassCamPart.Transparency = 1
        bypassCamPart.Anchored = true
        bypassCamPart.CanCollide = false
        local v5 = getChar()
        local humanoidRootPart_ = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart_ then
            bypassCamPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
        elseif bypassLastCF then
            bypassCamPart.CFrame = bypassLastCF
        else
            bypassCamPart.CFrame = CFrame.new()
        end

        bypassCamPart.Parent = Workspace
        currentCamera2.CameraSubject = bypassCamPart
        currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end
end

unlockCam = function()
    local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if currentCamera2 then
        local v5 = getChar()
        local humanoid_2 = v5 and v5:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoid_2 then
            currentCamera2.CameraSubject = humanoid_2
        end

        currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end

    if bypassCamPart then
        bypassCamPart:Destroy()
        bypassCamPart = nil
    end
end

runBypassCircle = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if not bypassLastCF then
        bypassLastCF = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
    end

    local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
    local now = tick()

    while tick() - now < 1 and bypassActive do
        if not localPlayer.Character or not humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
            return
        end
        local n = tick() * 12
        local n2 = math.cos(n) * 10000
        local n3 = math.sin(n) * 10000
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2 + Vector3.new(n2, -10000, n3)
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
    end

    if bypassActive and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2
    end
end

startBypass = function()
    if bypassActive then
        return
    end
    bypassActive = true
    local v5 = getChar()
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart_ then
        bypassLastCF = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
    end

    lockCam()
    enableFallenBypass()

    local v6 = coroutine.wrap(function()
        while bypassActive do
            if localPlayer.Character then
                runBypassCircle()
            else
                task.wait(0.5)
            end

            task.wait(0.0001)
        end
    end)

    bypassThread = v6
    bypassThread()
end

if not gucciId then
    gucciId = 0
end

stopBypass = function()
    bypassActive = false
    bypassThread = nil
    local v5 = getChar()
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if v5 and bypassLastCF and humanoidRootPart_ then
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = bypassLastCF
    end

    disableFallenBypass()
    unlockCam()
end

StartPlatformLoopV2 = function()
    while loopActiveV2 do
        local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

        if humanoidRootPart_ and currentCamera2 then
            if not platformPartV2 then
                local part2 = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
                platformPartV2 = part2
                platformPartV2.Name = "SkyBaseV2"
                platformPartV2.Anchored = true
                platformPartV2.Size = Vector3.new(1500, 2, 1500)
                platformPartV2.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1000000, 0)
                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -9999999
            end

            local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
            local cFrame3 = currentCamera2.CFrame
            currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
            currentCamera2.CFrame = cFrame3
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = platformPartV2.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            task.wait(loopIntervalV2)

            if not loopActiveV2 then
                local character_3 = localPlayer.Character
                local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_3 and character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart_2 then
                    humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = cFrame2
                end

                currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                break
            end

            local character_3 = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_3 and character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local v5 = humanoidRootPart_2

            if humanoidRootPart_2 then
                humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = cFrame2
                v5.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                v5.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            task.wait(loopIntervalV2)
            continue
        end

        task.wait(0.05)
    end

    local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if currentCamera2 then
        currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end
end

runDodgeLoop = function(arg)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    local humanoid_2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoid_2 then
        return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local v5 = getSafePos()
        humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(v5)
        humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while dodgeActive and arg.Parent do
            task.wait()
            local inPlot_2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

            if (not inPlot_2 or not inPlot_2.Value) and humanoid_2.Health > 0 then
                local v5 = getSafePos()
                humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(v5)
                humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
            end
        end
    end)

    local connection2 = nil

    connection2 = humanoid_2.Died:Connect(function()
        task.wait(2.8)

        while dodgeActive and arg.Parent do
            local inPlot_2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

            if not inPlot_2 or not inPlot_2.Value then
                for i = 1, 3 do
                    task.spawn(function()
                        if struggle then
                            struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            task.wait()
        end

        if connection2 then
            connection2:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
end

startAntiExplode = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    local result2 = v5 or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoidRootPart_ = result2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end

    if antiExplodeConn then
        antiExplodeConn:Disconnect()
    end

    local connection2 = Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "Part" and antiExplodeActive then
            if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoidRootPart_.Parent or not result2.Parent then
                return
            end

            if (child.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
                humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = true
                task.wait(0.01)
                local rightArm = result2:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                local ragdollLimbPart = rightArm and rightArm:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

                if ragdollLimbPart then
                    while ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide and antiExplodeActive do
                        task.wait(0.001)
                    end
                end

                if antiExplodeActive and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
                    humanoidRootPart_.Anchored = false
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    antiExplodeConn = connection2
end

startAntiBurn = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = v5:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not humanoid_2 or not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end
    v5.PrimaryPart = humanoidRootPart_

    if antiBurnConn then
        antiBurnConn:Disconnect()
    end

    local fireDebounce = humanoid_2:WaitForChild("FireDebounce", 5)

    if fireDebounce then
        local connection2 = fireDebounce.Changed:Connect(function(arg)
            if arg and antiBurnActive then
                local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                local plot2 = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("Plot2")
                local barrier = plot2 and plot2:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
                local plotBarrier = barrier and barrier:FindFirstChild("PlotBarrier")

                if plotBarrier and plotBarrier:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local n = plotBarrier.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 6, 0)
                    v5:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(n)
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    local firePlayerPart = v5:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart", true)

                    if firePlayerPart then
                        for i, child in ipairs(firePlayerPart:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("Sound") then
                                child:Stop()
                            end

                            if child:IsA("Light") or child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                                child.Enabled = false
                            end
                        end

                        if firePlayerPart:FindFirstChild("CanBurn") then
                            firePlayerPart.CanBurn.Value = false
                        end

                        if humanoid_2:FindFirstChild("FireDebounce") then
                            humanoid_2.FireDebounce.Value = false
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(0.6)

                    if v5 and v5.PrimaryPart and antiBurnActive then
                        v5:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        antiBurnConn = connection2
    end
end

paintPartsBackup = {}
paintConnections = {}

deleteAllPaintParts = function()
    for i, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
            local clone = descendant:Clone()
            clone.Archivable = true
            paintPartsBackup[descendant:GetDebugId()] = {clone = clone, parent = descendant.Parent}
            descendant:Destroy()
        end
    end
end

restorePaintParts = function()
    for k, v5 in pairs(paintPartsBackup) do
        if v5.clone and v5.parent then
            v5.clone.Parent = v5.parent
        end
    end

    paintPartsBackup = {}
end

watchNewPaintParts = function()
    table.insert(paintConnections, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
            task.defer(function()
                if descendant and descendant.Parent then
                    local clone = descendant:Clone()
                    clone.Archivable = true
                    paintPartsBackup[descendant:GetDebugId()] = {clone = clone, parent = descendant.Parent}
                    descendant:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end
    end))
end

disconnectWatchers = function()
    for i, v5 in ipairs(paintConnections) do
        if v5.Connected then
            v5:Disconnect()
        end
    end

    paintConnections = {}
end

setTouchQuery = function(canTouch)
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    local result2 = character_2 or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    if not result2 then
        return
    end

    for i, child in ipairs(result2:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Part") or child:IsA("BasePart") then
            child.CanTouch = canTouch
            child.CanQuery = canTouch
        end
    end
end

startAntiBlobKill = function()
    while antiBlobActive do
        local v5 = getChar()

        if v5 then
            local humanoid_2 = v5:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local humanoidRootPart_ = v5.HumanoidRootPart

            if humanoid_2 and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2.Health > 0 then
                humanoid_2.Sit = true
                humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

                if currentCamera2 then
                    local lookVector = currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector
                    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position, humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z))
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait()
    end
end

createTruePospart = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end

    if character_2:FindFirstChild("TruePositionPart") then
        return character_2.TruePositionPart
    end
    local part2 = Instance.new("Part")
    part2.Name = "TruePositionPart"
    part2.Anchored = true
    part2.CanCollide = false
    part2.Transparency = 1
    part2.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    part2.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -100, 0)
    part2.Parent = character_2
    return tpEnd
end

dropFromBlobs = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end
    local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")

    if plotItems then
        local v5, v6, v7 = pairs(plotItems:GetChildren())
        local v8 = v7
        local v9 = v5
        local v10 = v6

        while true do
            local v11, v12 = v9(v10, v8)
            if v11 == nil then
                break
            end
            v8 = v11

            if v12.Name ~= "PlayersInPlots" then
                for k, child in pairs(v12:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                        pcall(function()
                            local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = child:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local rightDetector = child:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

                            if blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and rightDetector then
                                local creatureDrop = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                                local rightWeld = rightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")

                                if creatureDrop and rightWeld then
                                    creatureDrop:FireServer(rightWeld, humanoidRootPart_)

                                    if struggle then
                                        struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end

                v8 = v11
            end
        end
    end

    local v5, v6, v7 = pairs(Players:GetPlayers())
    local v8 = v7
    local v9 = v5
    local v10 = v6

    while true do
        local v11, v12 = v9(v10, v8)
        if v11 == nil then
            break
        end
        v8 = v11
        local v13 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(v12.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

        if v13 then
            for k, child in pairs(v13:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    pcall(function()
                        local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = child:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                        local rightDetector = child:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")

                        if blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and rightDetector then
                            local creatureDrop = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            local rightWeld = rightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")

                            if creatureDrop and rightWeld then
                                creatureDrop:FireServer(rightWeld, humanoidRootPart_)

                                if struggle then
                                    struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            v8 = v11
        end
    end
end

setMassless = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end

    for k, child in pairs(character_2:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Massless then
            child.Massless = false
            dropFromBlobs()
        end
    end
end

moveRootAttachment = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local truePositionPart = character_2:FindFirstChild("TruePositionPart")

    if humanoidRootPart_ and truePositionPart then
        local rootAttachment = humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

        if rootAttachment then
            task.wait(0.2)
            rootAttachment.Parent = truePositionPart
        end
    end
end

restoreRootAttachment = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local truePositionPart = character_2:FindFirstChild("TruePositionPart")

    if humanoidRootPart_ and truePositionPart then
        local rootAttachment = truePositionPart:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")

        if rootAttachment then
            rootAttachment.Parent = humanoidRootPart_
        end

        truePositionPart:Destroy()
    end
end

antiblobLoop = function()
    while antiblob do
        pcall(function()
            if localPlayer.Character then
                createTruePospart()
                setMassless()
                local humanoidRootPart_ = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment") then
                    local truePositionPart = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("TruePositionPart")

                    if truePositionPart and not truePositionPart:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment") then
                        moveRootAttachment()
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end

spawnGucci = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    while inPlot and inPlot.Value and inOwnedPlot and not inOwnedPlot.Value and canSpawn and not canSpawn.Value do
        task.wait()
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local v5 = spawnToy
        local invokeServer = v5.InvokeServer
        local vector3 = arg3 or Vector3.new()
        invokeServer(v5, arg, arg2, vector3)
    end)

    local v5 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    if not v5 then
        return nil
    end
    local v6 = nil
    local connection2 = nil

    connection2 = v5.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == arg and child:IsA("Model") then
            v6 = child
            connection2:Disconnect()
        end
    end)

    local now = tick()

    while not v6 do
        if tick() - now > 2 then
            if connection2 then
                connection2:Disconnect()
            end

            return nil
        end

        task.wait()
    end

    return v6
end

sendChat = function(arg)
    local TextChatService2 = game:GetService("TextChatService")

    if TextChatService2 and TextChatService2.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
        local rbxGeneral = TextChatService2.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")

        if rbxGeneral then
            rbxGeneral:SendAsync(arg)
        end
    else
        local defaultChatSystemChatEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
        local sayMessageRequest = defaultChatSystemChatEvents and ReplicatedStorage.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")

        if sayMessageRequest then
            sayMessageRequest:FireServer(arg, "All")
        end
    end
end

SetNetworkOwner = function(arg)
    local v5 = HRP()

    if setNetOwner and v5 then
        pcall(function()
            setNetOwner:FireServer(arg, v5.CFrame)
        end)
    end
end

blobGrab = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v5 = arg:FindFirstChild(arg3 .. "Detector")
    local v6 = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild(arg3 .. "Weld")
    local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = arg:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
    local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript and blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")

    if v5 and v6 and creatureGrab then
        pcall(function()
            creatureGrab:FireServer(v5, arg2, v6)
        end)
    end
end

ungrab = function(arg)
    if destroyLine then
        pcall(function()
            destroyLine:FireServer(arg)
        end)
    end
end

blobKick = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v5 = HRP()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    blobGrab(arg, v5, arg3)
    task.wait(0.1)
    SetNetworkOwner(arg2)
    task.wait()
    arg2.CFrame = arg2.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
    ungrab(arg2)
    task.wait(0.1)
    blobGrab(arg, arg2, arg3)
end

shouldProcessPlayer = function(arg)
    if arg == localPlayer then
        return false
    end
    local inPlot_2 = arg:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
    if inPlot_2 and inPlot_2.Value then
        return false
    end
    local character_2 = arg.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return false
    end
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    local v5 = KickCooldown[arg.UserId]
    if v5 and os.clock() - v5 < COOLDOWN_TIME then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

removeAllForces = function(arg)
    for i, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BodyForce") or descendant:IsA("BodyVelocity") or descendant:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") or descendant:IsA("BodyGyro") or descendant:IsA("VectorForce") or descendant:IsA("LinearVelocity") or descendant:IsA("AngularVelocity") or descendant:IsA("AlignPosition") or descendant:IsA("AlignOrientation") then
            pcall(function()
                descendant:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
end

cleanForcesPeriodically = function(arg)
    task.spawn(function()
        for i = 1, 15 do
            if not arg or not arg.Parent then
                break
            end
            removeAllForces(arg)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

updateBlobman = function()
    local v5 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

    if v5 then
        local creatureBlobman = v5:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
        AuraBlobman = creatureBlobman
    else
        AuraBlobman = nil
    end
end

bkaAuraThread = nil

startAuraLoop = function()
    if bkaAuraThread then
        return
    end
    updateBlobman()

    local thread3 = task.spawn(function()
        local n, n2, v5, humanoid_2, v6, v7, v8, v9, v10, v11, v12, character_2, torso, humanoidRootPart_, n3
        n3 = 1

        while true do
            if n3 == 1 then
                n = 0
                n3 = 2
            elseif n3 == 2 then
                if OrionKickAura then
                    n3 = 3
                else
                    n3 = 18
                end
            elseif n3 == 3 then
                task.wait(0.05)
                n2 = n + 1
                n = n2

                if n2 >= 10 then
                    n3 = 4
                else
                    n3 = 5
                end
            elseif n3 == 4 then
                n = 0
                updateBlobman()
                n3 = 5
            elseif n3 == 5 then
                if AuraBlobman and AuraBlobman.Parent then
                    n3 = 6
                else
                    n3 = 2
                end
            elseif n3 == 6 then
                v5 = getChar()
                humanoid_2 = v5

                if v5 then
                    n3 = 7
                else
                    n3 = 8
                end
            elseif n3 == 7 then
                humanoid_2 = v5:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                n3 = 8
            elseif n3 == 8 then
                if humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Sit then
                    n3 = 9
                else
                    n3 = 2
                end
            elseif n3 == 9 then
                v6 = HRP()

                if v6 then
                    n3 = 10
                else
                    n3 = 2
                end
            elseif n3 == 10 then
                v7, v8, v9 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
                v10 = v9
                n3 = 11
            elseif n3 == 11 then
                v11, v12 = v7(v8, v10)

                if v11 == nil then
                    n3 = 2
                else
                    n3 = 12
                end
            elseif n3 == 12 then
                v10 = v11

                if shouldProcessPlayer(v12) then
                    n3 = 13
                else
                    n3 = 11
                end
            elseif n3 == 13 then
                character_2 = v12.Character
                torso = character_2

                if character_2 then
                    n3 = 14
                else
                    n3 = 16
                end
            elseif n3 == 14 then
                humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                torso = humanoidRootPart_

                if humanoidRootPart_ then
                    n3 = 16
                else
                    n3 = 15
                end
            elseif n3 == 15 then
                torso = character_2:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                n3 = 16
            elseif n3 == 16 then
                if torso and (torso.Position - v6.Position).Magnitude <= AuraRange then
                    n3 = 17
                else
                    n3 = 11
                end
            elseif n3 == 17 then
                KickCooldown[v12.UserId] = os.clock()
                task.spawn(blobKick, AuraBlobman, torso, "Left")
                cleanForcesPeriodically(character_2)
                n3 = 2
            elseif n3 == 18 then
                bkaAuraThread = nil
                return
            end
        end
    end)

    bkaAuraThread = thread3
end

stopGucci = function()
    gucciId = gucciId + 1

    if gucciBlob then
        pcall(function()
            if gucciBlob.Parent then
                gucciBlob:Destroy()
            end
        end)

        gucciBlob = nil
    end
end

runGucci = function(arg)
    stopGucci()
    gucciId = gucciId + 1
    local v5 = gucciId
    local result2 = arg or getChar() or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local v6 = result2
    local humanoid_2 = v6:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v6:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
    if not humanoid_2 or not humanoidRootPart_ then
        return
    end
    humanoid_2.Sit = true
    task.wait()
    humanoid_2.Sit = false

    task.spawn(function()
        local now = tick()

        while v5 == gucciId and tick() - now < 0.8 do
            for i, child in ipairs(v6:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                    child.Velocity = Vector3.new()
                end
            end

            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    local CreatureBlobman = spawnGucci("CreatureBlobman", humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5), Vector3.new(0, -15.716, 0))
    if not CreatureBlobman then
        return
    end
    gucciBlob = CreatureBlobman
    local head = CreatureBlobman:WaitForChild("Head", 3)
    local grabbableHitbox = CreatureBlobman:WaitForChild("GrabbableHitbox", 3)
    local vehicleSeat = CreatureBlobman:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 3)

    task.spawn(function()
        while v5 == gucciId and head and (not head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or head.PartOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name) do
            if grabbableHitbox then
                claimNetOwner(grabbableHitbox)
            end

            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    local flag8 = true

    task.spawn(function()
        local now = tick()

        while flag8 and v5 == gucciId and tick() - now < 0.4 do
            if CreatureBlobman and CreatureBlobman.Parent and vehicleSeat and vehicleSeat.Occupant ~= humanoid_2 then
                vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid_2)
            end

            task.wait()

            if v6 and humanoid_2 then
                humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            end
        end

        flag8 = false
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while flag8 and v5 == gucciId do
            ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 0.095)
            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    task.wait(0.5)
    if v5 ~= gucciId then
        return
    end
    humanoid_2.Sit = false
    CreatureBlobman.Name = "Gucci"

    for i, child in ipairs(CreatureBlobman:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            child.CanCollide = false
            child.CanTouch = false
            child.CanQuery = false
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while v5 == gucciId and CreatureBlobman and head do
            head.CFrame = CFrame.new(head.Position.X, 100000, head.Position.Z)
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
end

spawnTractor = function()
    local str = localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"

    local tbl4 = {
        "TractorGreen",
        CFrame.new(0, 5000000, 0),
        Vector3.new(0, 60, 0),
    }

    spawnToy:InvokeServer(unpack(tbl4))
    local v5 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(str)
    local v6 = v5 or Workspace:WaitForChild(str, 1.5)

    if v6 then
        local tractorGreen = v6:FindFirstChild("TractorGreen")
        local tractorGreen2 = tractorGreen or v6:WaitForChild("TractorGreen", 1.5)
        tractorObj = tractorGreen2

        if tractorObj then
            local vehicleSeat = tractorObj:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
            local vehicleSeat2 = vehicleSeat or tractorObj:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 1.5)
            tractorSeat = vehicleSeat2
            local main = tractorObj:FindFirstChild("Main")
            local main2 = main or tractorObj:WaitForChild("Main", 1.5)

            if main2 then
                main2.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 50000, 0)
                main2.Main.Main.Anchored = true
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

stopTractor = function()
    if tractorConn then
        tractorConn:Disconnect()
        tractorConn = nil
    end

    if tractorObj then
        pcall(function()
            ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(tractorObj)
            tractorObj:Destroy()
        end)

        tractorObj = nil
        tractorSeat = nil
    end
end

startTractor = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humanoid_3 = humanoid_2 or character_2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
    tractorHum = humanoid_3
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoidRootPart_2 = humanoidRootPart_ or character_2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
    tractorRoot = humanoidRootPart_2
    if not tractorHum or not tractorRoot then
        return
    end
    tractorPos = tractorRoot.Position

    if tractorSeat and tractorSeat:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
        tractorRoot.CFrame = tractorSeat.CFrame
        tractorSeat:Sit(tractorHum)
    end

    tractorHum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Jump"):Connect(function()
        if tractorHum.Jump and tractorHum.Sit then
            tractorFrames = 15
            tractorPos = tractorRoot.Position
        end
    end)

    if tractorConn then
        tractorConn:Disconnect()
    end

    local connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not tractorRoot or not tractorRoot.Parent or not tractorHum or not tractorHum.Parent then
            return
        end
        ReplicatedStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(tractorRoot, 0)

        if tractorFrames > 0 then
            tractorRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(tractorPos)
            tractorFrames = 1
        end
    end)

    tractorConn = connection2

    task.spawn(function()
        while tractorHum and tractorHum.Parent and tractorHum.Sit do
            task.wait(0.1)
        end

        task.wait(0.2)

        if tractorRoot and tractorRoot.Parent then
            tractorRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(tractorPos)
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)

        if autoGucciActiveTrain then
            local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not character_2 or not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
                continue
            end
            local map2 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local alwaysHereTweenedObjects = map2 and Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
            local train = alwaysHereTweenedObjects and alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Train")

            if not train then
                if not antiGucciConnectionTrain then
                    continue
                end

                pcall(function()
                    antiGucciConnectionTrain:Disconnect()
                    antiGucciConnectionTrain = nil
                end)

                notify("System", "Train lost, waiting...", 3)
                continue
            end

            if antiGucciConnectionTrain then
                continue
            end
            pcall(startAntiGucciTrain)
            notify("System", "Train detected, restarted.", 3)
        end
    end
end)

setupDeath = function(arg)
    local humanoid_2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

    if humanoid_2 then
        humanoid_2.Died:Connect(function()
            if antiGucciConnectionTrain then
                pcall(function()
                    antiGucciConnectionTrain:Disconnect()
                end)

                antiGucciConnectionTrain = nil
            end

            if jumpConnection then
                pcall(function()
                    jumpConnection:Disconnect()
                end)

                jumpConnection = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end

charAddedConn = nil

onDescAdded = function(arg)
    if arg.Name == "PartOwner" and _G.AutoAttacker then
        local str = tostring(arg.Value)
        local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(str)
        if not v5 or v5 == localPlayer then
            return
        end

        local function fn25()
            local character_2 = v5.Character
            local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoid_2 and humanoidRootPart_ then
                applySkyVelocity(humanoidRootPart_)

                for i = 1, 20 do
                    humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                    humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                    humanoid_2.Jump = true
                    humanoid_2.Sit = true
                end

                task.wait()
                destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
            end
        end

        for i = 1, 50 do
            if claimPlayerOwner(v5, fn25) then
                break
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end
end

startSnowball = function(arg)
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    if not v5 then
        return
    end

    while snowActive and v5 and v5.Parent do
        local character_2 = v5.Character
        local torso = character_2

        if character_2 then
            local upperTorso = character_2:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            torso = upperTorso or character_2:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        end

        if torso then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.random(-30, 30) / 100, math.random(-30, 30) / 100, math.random(-30, 30) / 100)
                    spawnToy:InvokeServer("BallSnowball", torso.CFrame * CFrame.new(vector3), Vector3.zero)
                end)
            end)

            local v6 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

            if v6 then
                for i, child in ipairs(v6:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "BallSnowball" and child.Parent then
                        local primaryPart = child.PrimaryPart
                        local basePart = primaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                        if basePart then
                            local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.random(-30, 30) / 100, math.random(-30, 30) / 100, math.random(-30, 30) / 100)
                            basePart.CFrame = torso.CFrame * CFrame.new(vector3)
                            basePart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            basePart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        else
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
end

runPallet = function(arg)
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    if not v5 or not v5.Character then
        return
    end
    local cframe = CFrame.new(0, 800000, 0)
    spawnToy:InvokeServer("PalletLightBrown", cframe, Vector3.zero)
    local v6, palletLightBrown, v7

    repeat
        v6 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
        palletLightBrown = v6 and v6:FindFirstChild("PalletLightBrown")
        v7 = palletLightBrown
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    until v7 or not palletActive

    if not v7 then
        return
    end
    local soundPart = v7:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
    local basePart = soundPart or v7:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not basePart then
        return
    end
    basePart.CanCollide = false
    basePart.Anchored = false

    local function fn25(arg2)
        setNetOwner:FireServer(arg2, arg2.CFrame)
        ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine:FireServer(arg2, Vector3.zero, arg2.Position, false)
        destroyLine:FireServer(arg2)
    end

    fn25(basePart)

    while palletActive and v5 and v5.Parent and v5.Character do
        local character_2 = v5.Character
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local torso = humanoidRootPart_ or character_2:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character_2:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

        if torso then
            basePart.CFrame = CFrame.new(torso.Position + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0))
            basePart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            basePart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            fn25(basePart)
            basePart.CanCollide = false
            task.wait(0.05)

            if torso and v5.Character then
                basePart.CFrame = CFrame.new(torso.Position)
                basePart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -400, 0)
                basePart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                fn25(basePart)
                basePart.CanCollide = true
            end

            task.wait(0.12)

            if torso and v5.Character then
                basePart.CFrame = CFrame.new(torso.Position + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0))
                basePart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
                basePart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                fn25(basePart)
                basePart.CanCollide = false
            end

            task.wait(0.08)
        else
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end

    if v7 and v7.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(v7)
        end)

        v7:Destroy()
    end
end

currentTarget = nil
currentSpamTask = nil
currentMainTask = nil
currentBodyPosition = nil
currentBodyGyro = nil
savedPosition = nil
dragging = false
grabStartTime = 0
checkStartTime = 0
isSpamActive = false
noclipConnection = nil

SetNoclip = function(arg)
    if noclipConnection then
        noclipConnection:Disconnect()
        noclipConnection = nil
    end

    if arg then
        local connection2 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if localPlayer.Character then
                for i, descendant in ipairs(localPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        descendant.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        noclipConnection = connection2
    end
end

SetNetworkOwnerForTarget = function(arg)
    if not arg then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        setNetOwner:FireServer(arg, arg.CFrame)
    end)
end

DoDestroyGrabLine = function(arg)
    if not arg then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        destroyLine:FireServer(arg)
    end)
end

CreateBodies = function(parent, position)
    if currentBodyPosition then
        currentBodyPosition:Destroy()
        currentBodyPosition = nil
    end

    if currentBodyGyro then
        currentBodyGyro:Destroy()
        currentBodyGyro = nil
    end

    local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
    currentBodyPosition = bodyPosition
    currentBodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000000, 100000000, 100000000)
    currentBodyPosition.P = 1000000
    currentBodyPosition.D = 1000
    currentBodyPosition.Position = position
    currentBodyPosition.Parent = parent
    local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    currentBodyGyro = bodyGyro
    currentBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(100000000, 100000000, 100000000)
    currentBodyGyro.P = 1000000
    currentBodyGyro.D = 1000
    currentBodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
    currentBodyGyro.Parent = parent
end

CleanupBodies = function()
    if currentBodyPosition then
        currentBodyPosition:Destroy()
        currentBodyPosition = nil
    end

    if currentBodyGyro then
        currentBodyGyro:Destroy()
        currentBodyGyro = nil
    end
end

StopAll = function()
    isSpamActive = false

    if currentSpamTask then
        task.cancel(currentSpamTask)
        currentSpamTask = nil
    end

    if currentMainTask then
        task.cancel(currentMainTask)
        currentMainTask = nil
    end

    CleanupBodies()
    SetNoclip(false)
    dragging = false
    grabStartTime = 0
    checkStartTime = 0
end

voidIsHigh = function(arg)
    local character_2 = arg.Character
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local flag8

    if humanoidRootPart_ then
        flag8 = false
    else
        flag8 = true
    end

    if not flag8 then
        flag8 = voidHeight < humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y
    end

    return flag8
end

voidNoCollide = function(arg)
    for i, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
            descendant.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
end

findBlob = function()
    local v5 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    local creatureBlobman = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman") or nil
    return creatureBlobman
end

spawnBlob = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    spawnToy:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", v5.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5), Vector3.new(0, -15, 0))
end

voidEnsureBlob = function()
    local v5 = findBlob()
    if v5 then
        return v5
    end
    spawnBlob()

    for i = 1, 11 do
        task.wait(1)
        local v6 = findBlob()
        if v6 then
            return v6
        end
    end

    return nil
end

voidSavePos = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart_ then
        v5:SetAttribute("OriginalPosition", humanoidRootPart_:GetPivot())
    end
end

voidGetPos = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    local attribute = v5 and v5:GetAttribute("OriginalPosition") or nil
    return attribute
end

voidReturnHome = function()
    local v5 = voidGetPos()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    local connection2 = nil

    connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local v6 = getChar()
        local humanoidRootPart_ = v6 and v6:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart_ then
            humanoidRootPart_:PivotTo(v5)

            if getgenv().originalFallenHeight then
                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = getgenv().originalFallenHeight
            end

            v6:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", false)
        end

        connection2:Disconnect()
    end)
end

voidModify = function(parent, arg)
    if not parent or not arg or arg.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local v5 = voidEnsureBlob()

    if v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") then
        local creatureDrop = v5.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")

        if creatureDrop then
            for i, descendant in ipairs(arg.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
                    creatureDrop:FireServer(descendant, descendant)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    arg.Sit = false
    arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
    arg:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
    arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
    local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg.Parent)

    if playerFromCharacter and playerFromCharacter:FindFirstChild("IsHeld") then
        playerFromCharacter.IsHeld.Value = false
    end

    local ragdolled = arg:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

    if ragdolled then
        ragdolled.Value = false
    end

    local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    local bodyAngularVelocity = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
    bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(10000000, -10000000, 10000000)
    bodyVelocity.P = 100
    bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 50), -50, math.random(-50, 50))
    bodyAngularVelocity.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(-10000000, -10000000, -10000000)
    bodyAngularVelocity.P = 1000000
    bodyAngularVelocity.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 300), math.random(-300, 300), math.random(-500, 500))
    bodyVelocity.Parent = parent
    bodyAngularVelocity.Parent = parent
    arg.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
    arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
    arg.RigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15

    task.delay(0.08, function()
        if bodyVelocity.Parent then
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
        end

        if bodyAngularVelocity.Parent then
            bodyAngularVelocity:Destroy()
        end
    end)
end

voidKill = function()
    local targetPlayerName = targetName ~= "" and targetName or _G.TargetPlayerName
    if targetPlayerName == "" then
        return
    end
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPlayerName)
    local character_2 = v5 and v5.Character
    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local head = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not v5 or not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoid_2 or not head then
        return
    end

    if voidIsHigh(v5) then
        return
    end

    if v5:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and v5.InPlot.Value then
        return
    end

    if humanoid_2:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
        return
    end
    local v6 = getChar()
    local humanoidRootPart_2 = v6 and v6:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not v6 or not humanoidRootPart_2 then
        return
    end

    if not v6:GetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos") then
        voidSavePos()
    end

    v6:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", true)
    getgenv().originalFallenHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
    Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0 / 0)
    humanoidRootPart_2:PivotTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + voidOffset))
    voidNoCollide(character_2)
    setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, humanoidRootPart_.CFrame)
    task.wait()
    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
    task.wait()

    if head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and head.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
        task.wait()
        voidModify(humanoidRootPart_, humanoid_2)
    end

    voidReturnHome()
end

startVoidKill = function()
    if voidConn then
        voidConn:Disconnect()
    end

    local connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(voidKill)
    voidConn = connection2
end

stopVoidKill = function()
    if voidConn then
        voidConn:Disconnect()
        voidConn = nil
    end
end

runLoopKickBlob = function(arg)
    task.spawn(function()
        if not Players:FindFirstChild(arg) then
            return
        end
        local v5 = getChar()
        local humanoid_2 = v5 and v5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local seatPart = humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.SeatPart

        if seatPart and seatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            local parent = seatPart.Parent
            local humanoidRootPart_ = parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or parent.PrimaryPart
            local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = parent:WaitForChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
            local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:WaitForChild("CreatureGrab")
            local creatureDrop = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:WaitForChild("CreatureDrop")
            local rightDetector = parent:WaitForChild("RightDetector")
            local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
            local flag8 = false
            local n = 0
            local n2 = 0
            notify("Loop Kick Blob", arg .. " を攻撃開始", 4)

            while loopKickBlobActive do
                local v6 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
                if not v6 then
                    break
                end
                local v7 = getChar()
                local humanoid_3 = v7 and v7:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                local v8 = humanoid_3
                local seatPart2 = humanoid_3 and v8.SeatPart
                local v9 = seatPart2

                if seatPart2 and seatPart2.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    local parent2 = v9.Parent
                    local humanoidRootPart_2 = parent2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local primaryPart = humanoidRootPart_2 or parent2.PrimaryPart
                    humanoidRootPart_ = primaryPart
                    local character_2 = v6.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart_3 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local humanoid_4 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    if humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoid_4 and humanoid_4.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart_ then
                        humanoidRootPart_3.Velocity = Vector3.zero

                        if flag8 then
                            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2
                            humanoidRootPart_.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            local cFrame3 = cFrame2 * CFrame.new(0, 23, 0)
                            humanoidRootPart_3.CFrame = cFrame3
                            humanoid_4.PlatformStand = true
                            humanoid_4.Sit = true

                            if tick() - n2 >= 0.002 then
                                n2 = tick()

                                pcall(function()
                                    setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_3, cFrame3)
                                    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                    local rightWeld = rightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")
                                    local weld = rightWeld or rightDetector:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")

                                    if weld then
                                        creatureDrop:FireServer(weld)
                                        creatureGrab:FireServer(rightDetector, humanoidRootPart_3, weld)
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        else
                            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_3.CFrame
                            humanoidRootPart_.Velocity = Vector3.zero

                            if tick() - n2 >= 0.002 then
                                n2 = tick()

                                pcall(function()
                                    humanoid_4.PlatformStand = true
                                    humanoid_4.Sit = true
                                    setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_3, humanoidRootPart_.CFrame)
                                    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                end)
                            end

                            if n == 0 then
                                n = tick()
                            end

                            if tick() - n > 0.35 then
                                flag8 = true
                                n = 0
                                humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2
                                humanoidRootPart_.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        flag8 = false
                        n = 0
                    end

                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    continue
                end

                notify("Loop Kick Blob", "Blobから降りました。停止します。", 4)
                loopKickBlobActive = false
                break
            end

            if humanoidRootPart_ then
                humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2
                humanoidRootPart_.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            return
        end

        notify("Loop Kick Blob", "CreatureBlobmanに乗ってからONにしてください", 5)
    end)
end

getMountedBlob = function()
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = v5.Humanoid
    if humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.SeatPart and humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
        return humanoid_2.SeatPart.Parent
    end
    return nil
end

grabBlobArm = function(arg, arg2)
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5 and v5.Character and v5.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoidRootPart_ and arg2 and arg2:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") then
        local creatureGrab = arg2.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
        local rightDetector = arg2:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
        local rightWeld = rightDetector and rightDetector:FindFirstChild("RightWeld")

        if creatureGrab and rightWeld then
            creatureGrab:FireServer(localPlayer, humanoidRootPart_, rightWeld)
        end
    end
end

runBlobKick = function(arg)
    local v5 = getChar()
    if not v5 then
        return
    end
    local humanoid_2 = v5.Humanoid
    local humanoidRootPart_ = v5.HumanoidRootPart
    local v6 = getMountedBlob()
    local v7 = v6 or getOrSpawnBlob()
    if not v7 then
        return
    end
    task.wait(0.12)
    local vehicleSeat = v7:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

    if vehicleSeat then
        vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid_2)
        task.wait(0.12)
    end

    local v8 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    local humanoidRootPart_2 = v8 and v8.Character and v8.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoidRootPart_2 then
        return
    end
    local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
    local primaryPart = v7.PrimaryPart
    local cFrame3 = primaryPart and v7.PrimaryPart.CFrame
    local cFrame4 = humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame
    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame4
    task.wait(0.12)

    for i = 1, 15 do
        task.wait()
        setNetOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_2, humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame)
        humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = cFrame4 * CFrame.new(0, 40, 0)
    end

    task.wait(0.13)
    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_2)
    task.wait(0.13)
    grabBlobArm(arg, v7)
    grabBlobArm(arg, v7)
    grabBlobArm(arg, v7)

    task.delay(0.55, function()
        if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2
        end

        if v7 and v7.Parent and v7.PrimaryPart and cFrame3 then
            v7:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame3)
        end
    end)
end

filterActiveTarget = function(arg) end

monitorBlobKick = function(arg)
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    if not v5 then
        return
    end

    if blobKickConn then
        pcall(function()
            blobKickConn:Disconnect()
        end)

        blobKickConn = nil
    end

    local connection2 = v5.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if blobKickActive then
            task.wait(0.75)
            runBlobKick(arg)
        end
    end)

    blobKickConn = connection2
end

TP_WAIT = 0.08
GRAB_WAIT = 0.1
RETRY_WAIT = 0.01
MAX_RETRIES = 5
SELECTED_AURA_RANGE = 40
LOOP_DELAY = 0.5

BlobmanKill = {
    isSelectedKill = false,
    selectedPlayer = nil,
    selectedKillConnection = nil,
    selectedPlayerConnection = nil,
    selectedKillAuraConnection = nil,
    isProcessing = false,
}

GetSeatedBlobman = function()
    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
    if not character_2 then
        return nil
    end
    local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 then
        return nil
    end
    local seatPart = humanoid_2.SeatPart
    if not seatPart or not seatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
        return nil
    end
    local parent = seatPart

    while parent do
        if parent:IsA("Model") and parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            return parent
        end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end

    return nil
end

BlobmanKill.KillPlayer = function(arg)
    if not arg or not arg.Character then
        return false
    end
    local humanoid_2 = arg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid_2 then
        return false
    end

    for i = 1, MAX_RETRIES do
        if pcall(function()
            humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        end) and humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
            return true
        end

        task.wait(RETRY_WAIT)
    end

    return false
end

BlobmanKill.GrabRelease = function(arg, arg2)
    if not arg or not arg2 then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = arg:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")

        if blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript then
            local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
            local creatureRelease = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease")
            local leftDetector = arg:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")

            if creatureGrab and creatureRelease and leftDetector then
                local leftWeld = leftDetector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                local weld = leftWeld or leftDetector:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                creatureGrab:FireServer(leftDetector, arg2, weld)
                creatureRelease:FireServer(weld)
            end
        end
    end)
end

BlobmanKill.ProcessPlayer = function(arg, arg2)
    if BlobmanKill.isProcessing then
        return false
    end
    local v5 = GetSeatedBlobman()
    if not v5 then
        return false
    end
    BlobmanKill.isProcessing = true

    local ok, result2 = pcall(function()
        if not arg or not arg.Character then
            return false
        end
        local humanoid_2 = arg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid_2 or humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
            return false
        end
        local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoidRootPart_ then
            return false
        end
        local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
        if not character_2 then
            return
        end
        local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoidRootPart_2 then
            return false
        end
        local cFrame2 = arg2 or humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame
        local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local assemblyAngularVelocity = humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
        humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        task.wait(TP_WAIT)
        humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        v5 = GetSeatedBlobman()

        if v5 then
            BlobmanKill.GrabRelease(v5, humanoidRootPart_)
            task.wait(GRAB_WAIT)
        end

        if humanoidRootPart_2 and humanoidRootPart_2.Parent then
            humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = cFrame2
            local vector3 = assemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
            humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vector3
            local vector4 = assemblyAngularVelocity or Vector3.zero
            humanoidRootPart_2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = vector4
            humanoidRootPart_2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            humanoidRootPart_2.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        return true
    end)

    BlobmanKill.isProcessing = false
    local v6 = ok and result2
    return v6
end

StartSelectedKillAura = function()
    if BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection = nil
    end

    if not BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer then
        return
    end

    BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill or not BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer then
            return
        end
        local v5 = GetSeatedBlobman()
        if not v5 then
            return
        end
        local selectedPlayer = BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer
        if not selectedPlayer.Parent then
            return
        end
        local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
        if not character_2 then
            return
        end
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoidRootPart_ then
            return
        end
        local character_3 = selectedPlayer.Character
        if not character_3 then
            return
        end
        local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoidRootPart_2 then
            return
        end

        if (humanoidRootPart_.Position - humanoidRootPart_2.Position).Magnitude <= SELECTED_AURA_RANGE then
            BlobmanKill.KillPlayer(selectedPlayer)
            BlobmanKill.GrabRelease(v5, humanoidRootPart_2)
        end
    end)
end

BlobmanKill.StartSelectedKillLoop = function()
    if BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection = nil
    end

    if BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection = nil
    end

    if not BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer then
        return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill do
            if GetSeatedBlobman() and BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer and BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer.Parent then
                local character_2 = BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer.Character
                local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and not BlobmanKill.isProcessing then
                    pcall(BlobmanKill.ProcessPlayer, BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer)
                end
            end

            task.wait(LOOP_DELAY)
        end
    end)

    BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection = BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character_2)
        if BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill then
            if not GetSeatedBlobman() then
                return
            end
            local character_3 = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_3 and character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.CFrame

            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(4)
                if not GetSeatedBlobman() then
                    return
                end
                local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local humanoid_3 = humanoid_2 or character_2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
                local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoidRootPart_3 = humanoidRootPart_2 or character_2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)

                if humanoid_3 and humanoidRootPart_3 and BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill and cFrame2 then
                    pcall(BlobmanKill.ProcessPlayer, BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer, cFrame2)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    StartSelectedKillAura()
end

StopSelectedKill = function()
    BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill = false

    if BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillConnection = nil
    end

    if BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedPlayerConnection = nil
    end

    if BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection then
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection:Disconnect()
        BlobmanKill.selectedKillAuraConnection = nil
    end
end

runBlobKill = function(arg, arg2)
    if not arg then
        if not arg2 then
            Rayfield:Notify({
                Title = "エラー",
                Content = "ターゲットを選択してください",
                Duration = 2,
            })
        end

        return
    end

    if not BlobmanKill.ProcessPlayer(arg) and not arg2 then
        Rayfield:Notify({
            Title = "エラー",
            Content = "処理に失敗しました。ブロブマンに乗っていないか、ターゲットが不正です。",
            Duration = 2,
        })
    end
end

getBlobOrGucciPart = function(arg)
    if not arg then
        return nil
    end
    local tbl4 = {}
    local v5 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(arg.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

    if v5 then
        table.insert(tbl4, v5)
    end

    local v6, v7 = getHomePlot(arg)

    if v6 and v7 then
        table.insert(tbl4, v7)
    end

    local v8, v9, v10 = ipairs(tbl4)
    local v11 = v10
    local v12 = v8
    local v13 = v9

    while true do
        local v14, v15 = v12(v13, v11)
        if v14 == nil then
            break
        end

        for i, child in ipairs(v15:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" and child.Name ~= "Gucci" then
                continue
            end
            local humanoid_2 = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
                continue
            end
            local grabbableHitbox = child:FindFirstChild("GrabbableHitbox")
            local head = grabbableHitbox or child:FindFirstChild("Head") or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if head and head.Parent then
                return head
            end
        end

        v11 = v14
    end

    return nil
end

runFtapKill = function(arg)
    local v5 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
    if not v5 then
        return
    end

    while ftapLoopKillActive and v5 and v5.Parent do
        local character_2 = v5.Character
        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if isValid(v5) and not isInSafeZone(v5) then
            local v6 = getCF()

            if changePriority(2) and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 then
                for i = 1, 50 do
                    if not ftapLoopKillActive then
                        break
                    end
                    startFloat()
                    claimNetOwner(humanoidRootPart_)

                    if isValid(v5) and not isInSafeZone(v5) and not checkPlayerOwner(v5) and humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude <= 500 then
                        task.wait()
