                        if humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y <= -12 then
                            tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)), 2)
                        else
                            tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)), 2)
                        end

                        humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                        humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                        humanoid_2.Jump = true
                        humanoid_2.Sit = false
                        continue
                    end

                    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
                    applySkyVelocity(humanoidRootPart_)
                    break
                end

                changePriority(0)
            end

            if v6 then
                tpSimple(v6)
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.2)
    end

    stopFloat()
    local v6 = getCF()

    if v6 then
        tpSimple(v6)
    end
end

isBringing = false
BringToggleUI = nil

FWC_Ragdoll = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local v5 = arg:FindFirstChild(arg2)
    local v6 = v5

    if not v5 then
        local waitForChild = arg.WaitForChild
        local n = arg3 or 5
        v6 = waitForChild(arg, arg2, n)
    end

    return v6
end

grab_Ragdoll = function(arg)
    if setNetOwner then
        setNetOwner:FireServer(arg, arg.CFrame)
    end
end

FindPlot_Ragdoll = function()
    local name = localPlayer.Name
    local plots = Workspace.Plots
    local plotItems = Workspace.PlotItems
    local n = 0

    while true do
        local n2 = n + 1
        if n2 > 5 then
            break
        end
        n = n2
        local str = "Plot" .. n2

        if plots:FindFirstChild(str) and plots[str]:FindFirstChild("PlotSign") then
            local children = plots[str].PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()

            for i, child in ipairs(children) do
                if child.Value == name then
                    return plotItems:FindFirstChild(str)
                end
            end

            n = n2
        end
    end

    return false
end

toy_spawn_Ragdoll = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
    local inPlot_2 = localPlayer:WaitForChild("InPlot")
    local inOwnedPlot_2 = localPlayer:WaitForChild("InOwnedPlot")
    local canSpawnToy2 = localPlayer:WaitForChild("CanSpawnToy")

    while inPlot_2.Value and not inOwnedPlot_2.Value and not canSpawnToy2.Value do
        task.wait()
    end

    if typeof(arg3) == "string" then
        if string.lower(arg3) == "headup" then
            task.spawn(function()
                spawnToy:InvokeServer(arg, CFrame.new(arg2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), arg2.Position) * CFrame.Angles(math.pi, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
            end)
        end
    elseif arg3 then
        task.spawn(function()
            spawnToy:InvokeServer(arg, arg2, arg3)
        end)
    else
        task.spawn(function()
            spawnToy:InvokeServer(arg, arg2, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
        end)
    end

    local v5 = nil
    local flag8 = nil
    local v6 = Workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

    if inOwnedPlot_2.Value then
        local v7 = FindPlot_Ragdoll()

        if v7 then
            v7.ChildAdded:Once(function(child)
                if child.Name == arg and child:IsA("Model") then
                    v5 = child
                end
            end)

            flag8 = true
        end
    else
        v6.ChildAdded:Once(function(child)
            if child.Name == arg and child:IsA("Model") then
                v5 = child
            end
        end)

        flag8 = false
    end

    local now = tick()

    while not v5 do
        if tick() - now >= 3 then
            return false
        end
        task.wait()
    end

    return v5, flag8
end

prepareRagdollToy = function()
    local v5 = Workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

    if not v5:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll") then
        local PalletLightBrown = toy_spawn_Ragdoll("PalletLightBrown", FWC_Ragdoll(localPlayer.Character, "Head"), "headup")
        if not PalletLightBrown then
            return nil
        end
        local soundPart = PalletLightBrown:WaitForChild("SoundPart")

        while soundPart and not soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value ~= localPlayer.Name do
            task.spawn(grab_Ragdoll, soundPart)
            task.wait()
        end

        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bodyVelocity.Parent = soundPart
        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
        bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)

        for k, child in pairs(PalletLightBrown:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                child.Transparency = 1
                child.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
            end
        end

        if soundPart:FindFirstChild("Wood") then
            soundPart.Wood:Destroy()
        end

        PalletLightBrown.Name = "Ragdoll"

        pcall(function()
            local v6 = nil

            for k, child in pairs(v5:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "Ragdoll" then
                    v6 = k
                end
            end

            for i, child in ipairs(localPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "PalletLightBrown" and i == v6 then
                    local viewItemButton = child.ViewItemButton
                    viewItemButton.Text = "RAGDOLL"
                    viewItemButton.TextScaled = true
                    viewItemButton.LowResImage.Image = ""
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    return v5:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll")
end

kickAll = function()

startLag = function()
    if lagEnabled then
        return
    end
    lagEnabled = true

    local thread3 = coroutine.create(function()
        if not grabEvents then
            return
        end
        local createGrabLine2 = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
        if not createGrabLine2 then
            return
        end

        while lagEnabled do
            local spawnLocation = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
            local spawn_ = spawnLocation or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

            if not spawn_ then
                local v5 = getChar()
                spawn_ = v5 and getChar().HumanoidRootPart
            end

            if spawn_ then
                local n = math.random(-9000000000, 9000000000)
                local n2 = math.random(-9000000000, 9000000000)

                local tbl4 = {
                    CFrame.new(n, 0, n2),
                    CFrame.new(-n, 0, -n2),
                    CFrame.new(n, 0, -n2),
                    CFrame.new(-n, 0, n2),
                }

                for i, v5 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                    createGrabLine2:FireServer(spawn_, v5)
                end
            end

            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    lagThread = thread3
    coroutine.resume(lagThread)
end

stopLag = function()
    if not lagEnabled then
        return
    end
    lagEnabled = false

    if lagThread then
        coroutine.close(lagThread)
        lagThread = nil
    end
end

onCharAdded = function(arg)
    setupDeath(arg)

    if charAddedConn then
        charAddedConn:Disconnect()
        charAddedConn = nil
    end

    local connection2 = arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(onDescAdded)
    charAddedConn = connection2

    if autoGucciActiveTrain then
        task.spawn(function()
            if arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) then
                task.wait(1)

                if autoGucciActiveTrain then
                    notify("System", "Respawned, Auto Gucci active", 3)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if tractorEnabled then
        stopTractor()

        task.spawn(function()
            local humanoid_2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
            local humanoidRootPart_ = arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)

            if tractorEnabled and humanoid_2 and humanoidRootPart_ and spawnTractor() and tractorObj then
                startTractor()
            end
        end)
    end
end

        end
    end,
})

        end
        local v18, v19, v20 = ipairs(plots:GetChildren())
        local v21 = v20
        local v22 = v18
        local v23 = v19

        while true do
            local v24, v25 = v22(v23, v21)
            if v24 == nil then
                break
            end
            v21 = v24
            local barrier = v25:FindFirstChild("Barrier")

            if barrier then
                for i, child in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "PlotBarrier" then
                        local canCollide

                        if arg then
                            canCollide = false
                        else
                            canCollide = true
                        end

                        child.CanCollide = canCollide
                    end
                end

                v21 = v24
            end
        end
    end,
})

v8:CreateSection("バナナ")
AutoDeleteLegsActive = false
DeleteLegsConnection = nil

PerformLegDeletion = function(arg)
    task.wait(0.5)
    if not AutoDeleteLegsActive then
        return
    end
    local humanoidRootPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid_2 = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local torso = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    local leftLeg = arg:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
    local rightLeg = arg:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

    if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 and torso and leftLeg and rightLeg then
        local fallenPartsDestroyHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
        local cFrame2 = torso.CFrame
        Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
        local characterEvents2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CharacterEvents")
        local ragdollRemote2 = characterEvents2 and characterEvents2:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote")

        if ragdollRemote2 then
            ragdollRemote2:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_, 2)
        end

        task.wait(0.5)

        if leftLeg and rightLeg then
            rightLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
            leftLeg.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
        end

        task.wait(0.3)

        if torso then
            torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -9970, 0)
        end

        task.wait(0.5)

        if torso then
            torso.CFrame = cFrame2
        end

        task.wait(0.5)
        Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fallenPartsDestroyHeight

        task.spawn(function()
            while AutoDeleteLegsActive and arg.Parent and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and not arg:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") and not arg:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") do
                pcall(function()
                    local controlsGui = localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ControlsGui")

                    if controlsGui and controlsGui:FindFirstChild("PCFrame") and controlsGui.PCFrame:FindFirstChild("Stand") then
                        if controlsGui.PCFrame.Stand.Visible == false then
                            humanoid_2.HipHeight = 2
                        else
                            humanoid_2.HipHeight = 0
                        end
                    end
                end)

                task.wait()
            end
        end)
    end
end

v8:CreateToggle({
    Name = "アンチバナナ",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "Anti Banana",
    Callback = function(arg)
        AutoDeleteLegsActive = arg

        if arg then
            if localPlayer.Character then
                task.spawn(function()
                    PerformLegDeletion(localPlayer.Character)
                end)
            end

            if not DeleteLegsConnection then
                local connection3 = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character_2)
                    if AutoDeleteLegsActive then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            PerformLegDeletion(character_2)
                        end)
                    end
                end)

                DeleteLegsConnection = connection3
            end
        elseif DeleteLegsConnection then
            DeleteLegsConnection:Disconnect()
            DeleteLegsConnection = nil
        end
    end,
})

v8:CreateSection("アンチPCLD")
local flag8 = false

v8:CreateToggle({
    Name = "PCLD ブレイク",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "AutoPCLDBreakSmart",
    Callback = function(arg)
        flag8 = arg

        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                local localPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                local flag9 = true

                while flag8 do
                    local character_2 = localPlayer2.Character or localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                    local humanoidRootPart_2 = humanoidRootPart_
                    local humanoid_2 = character_2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                    local humanoid_3 = humanoid_2

                    if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 then
                        if humanoid_2.Health <= 0 then
                            character_2 = localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                            humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                            humanoid_3 = character_2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                        end

                        if humanoidRootPart_2 and humanoid_3 then
                            if flag9 then
                                local cFrame2 = humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame
                                humanoidRootPart_2.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_2.Position.X, 50000, humanoidRootPart_2.Position.Z)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                if not flag8 then
                                    break
                                end
                                humanoid_3.Health = 0
                                local result3 = localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                                if not flag8 then
                                    break
                                end
                                local humanoidRootPart_3 = result3:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                                local humanoid_4 = result3:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

                                if humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoid_4 then
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    if not flag8 then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    humanoidRootPart_3.CFrame = cFrame2
                                    task.wait(0.05)
                                    if not flag8 then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    humanoid_4.Health = 0
                                end

                                flag9 = false
                            else
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                if not flag8 then
                                    break
                                end

                                if humanoid_3 then
                                    humanoid_3.Health = 0

                                    pcall(function()
                                        character_2:BreakJoints()
                                    end)
                                end
                            end

                            character_2 = localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                            if not flag8 then
                                break
                            end
                            local humanoid_4 = character_2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

                            if humanoid_4 then
                                humanoid_4.Died:Wait()
                            end

                            continue
                        end

                        continue
                    end

                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

v8:CreateToggle({
    Name = "アンチキック",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "ShurikenAntiKickSelfToggle",
    Callback = function(shurikenAntiKickSelf)
        _G.ShurikenAntiKickSelf = shurikenAntiKickSelf

        if shurikenAntiKickSelf then
            local function fn25()
                local v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                local menuToys2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
                local destroyToy2 = menuToys2 and ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")

                if v18 and destroyToy2 then
                    for k, child in pairs(v18:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "AntiKick" or child.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then
                            pcall(function()
                                destroyToy2:FireServer(child)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                local setNetworkOwner2, stickyPartEvent2, spawnToyRemoteFunction2, canSpawnToy2, fn26, fn27, fn28, fn29, v18, ninjaShuriken, v19, v20, plotSign, n
                n = 1

                while true do
                    if n == 1 then
                        setNetworkOwner2 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                        stickyPartEvent2 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
                        spawnToyRemoteFunction2 = ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction
                        canSpawnToy2 = localPlayer:WaitForChild("CanSpawnToy")

                        fn26 = function()
                            if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                return localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                            end
                            return localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait():WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        end

                        fn27 = function()
                            if not Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                                return false
                            end
                            local v21, v22, v23 = pairs(Workspace.Plots:GetChildren())
                            local v24 = v23
                            local v25 = v21
                            local v26 = v22

                            while true do
                                local v27, v28 = v25(v26, v24)
                                if v27 == nil then
                                    break
                                end
                                v24 = v27
                                local plotSign2 = v28:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                                local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign2 and plotSign2:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                                if not thisPlotsOwners then
                                    continue
                                end

                                for k, child in pairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
                                    if child.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                        local v29 = Workspace.PlotItems:FindFirstChild(v28.Name)
                                        if v29 then
                                            return true, v29
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                v24 = v27
                            end

                            return false
                        end

                        fn28 = function(arg)
                            if not arg or not arg:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                                return
                            end
                            local v21 = fn26()
                            if not v21 then
                                return
                            end

                            if arg:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") and (not arg.SoundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or arg.SoundPart.PartOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name) then
                                setNetworkOwner2:FireServer(arg.SoundPart, arg.SoundPart.CFrame)
                            end

                            local firePlayerPart = v21:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
                            local firePlayerPart2 = firePlayerPart or v21:WaitForChild("FirePlayerPart", 5)

                            if firePlayerPart2 then
                                stickyPartEvent2:FireServer(arg.StickyPart, firePlayerPart2, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(90)))
                            end

                            for k, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
                                if child.Name == "Pyramid" then
                                    child.CanTouch = false
                                    child.CanCollide = false
                                    child.CanQuery = false
                                    child.Transparency = 0

                                    if not child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
                                        Instance.new("Highlight", child).FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    end
                                elseif child.Name == "Main" then
                                    child.CanTouch = false
                                    child.CanCollide = false
                                    child.CanQuery = false
                                    child.Transparency = 0

                                    if not child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
                                        Instance.new("Highlight", child).FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                    end
                                elseif child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    child.CanTouch = false
                                    child.CanCollide = false
                                    child.CanQuery = false
                                    child.Transparency = 1
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        fn29 = function(arg)
                            local now = tick()

                            while not canSpawnToy2.Value do
                                if not _G.ShurikenAntiKickSelf or tick() - now > 5 then
                                    return nil
                                end
                                task.wait(0.1)
                            end

                            local v21 = fn26()

                            if v21 then
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        spawnToyRemoteFunction2:InvokeServer(arg, v21.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 12, 20), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
                                    end)
                                end)
                            end

                            local v22, v23 = fn27()
                            local v24 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                            if v22 and v23 then
                                return v23:WaitForChild(arg, 2)
                            end

                            if not Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) and v24 then
                                return v24:WaitForChild(arg, 2)
                            end
                            return nil
                        end

                        n = 2
                    elseif n == 2 then
                        if _G.ShurikenAntiKickSelf then
                            n = 3
                        else
                            n = 21
                        end
                    elseif n == 3 then
                        task.wait(0.005)

                        if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            n = 4
                        else
                            n = 2
                        end
                    elseif n == 4 then
                        v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        ninjaShuriken = v18

                        if ninjaShuriken then
                            n = 5
                        else
                            n = 6
                        end
                    elseif n == 5 then
                        ninjaShuriken = v18:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
                        n = 6
                    elseif n == 6 then
                        if Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                            n = 7
                        else
                            n = 11
                        end
                    elseif n == 7 then
                        v19, v20 = fn27()

                        if v19 and v20 and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(v20.Name) then
                            n = 8
                        else
                            n = 11
                        end
                    elseif n == 8 then
                        plotSign = Workspace.Plots[v20.Name]:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

                        if plotSign and plotSign.ThisPlotsOwners.Value.TimeRemainingNum.Value > 89 then
                            n = 9
                        else
                            n = 11
                        end
                    elseif n == 9 then
                        ninjaShuriken = fn29("NinjaShuriken")

                        if ninjaShuriken == nil then
                            n = 2
                        else
                            n = 10
                        end
                    elseif n == 10 then
                        ninjaShuriken.Name = "AntiKick"
                        fn28(ninjaShuriken)
                        n = 11
                    elseif n == 11 then
                        if ninjaShuriken then
                            n = 15
                        else
                            n = 12
                        end
                    elseif n == 12 then
                        if Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                            n = 2
                        else
                            n = 13
                        end
                    elseif n == 13 then
                        ninjaShuriken = fn29("NinjaShuriken")

                        if ninjaShuriken == nil then
                            n = 2
                        else
                            n = 14
                        end
                    elseif n == 14 then
                        ninjaShuriken.Name = "AntiKick"

                        if ninjaShuriken then
                            n = 15
                        else
                            n = 2
                        end
                    elseif n == 15 then
                        if ninjaShuriken and ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and ninjaShuriken.StickyPart.CanTouch == true then
                            n = 16
                        else
                            n = 17
                        end
                    elseif n == 16 then
                        fn28(ninjaShuriken)
                        ninjaShuriken.Name = "AntiKick"
                        n = 17
                    elseif n == 17 then
                        task.wait(0.3)

                        if ninjaShuriken and _G.ShurikenAntiKickSelf and ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and ninjaShuriken.StickyPart.CanTouch ~= false and localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - ninjaShuriken.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude < 20 then
                            n = 15
                        else
                            n = 18
                        end
                    elseif n == 18 then
                        if ninjaShuriken and ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - ninjaShuriken.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude < 20 then
                            n = 20
                        else
                            n = 19
                        end
                    elseif n == 19 then
                        fn25()
                        n = 20
                    elseif n == 20 then
                        pcall(function()
                            repeat
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            until not _G.ShurikenAntiKickSelf or not localPlayer.Character or not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not ninjaShuriken or not ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or not ninjaShuriken.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld") or not ninjaShuriken.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1

                            if not ninjaShuriken or not ninjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not ninjaShuriken.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld").Part1 then
                                fn25()
                            end
                        end)

                        n = 2
                    elseif n == 21 then
                        fn25()
                        return
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            (function()
                local v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                local menuToys2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
                local destroyToy2 = menuToys2 and ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")

v8:CreateToggle({
    Name = "アンチキル",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "AntiKillHamburgerToggle",
    Callback = function(arg)
        antiKillLoopActive = arg

        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                local spawnToyRemoteFunction2 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")

                while antiKillLoopActive do
                    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart_ then
                        local v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        local foodHamburger = v18 and v18:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger")
                        local v19, v20, v21 = pairs(Workspace:GetChildren())
                        local v22 = v21
                        local v23 = v19
                        local v24 = v20

                        while true do
                            local v25, v26 = v23(v24, v22)
                            if v25 == nil then
                                break
                            end
                            v22 = v25

                            if v26.Name == "Shuriken" and v26:IsA("Model") then
                                for k, descendant in pairs(v26:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        descendant.CanCollide = false
                                        descendant.Massless = true
                                    end
                                end

                                v22 = v25
                            end
                        end

                        if foodHamburger and foodHamburger.Parent then
                            local holdPart = foodHamburger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")

                            if holdPart then
                                for k, descendant in pairs(foodHamburger:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        descendant.CanCollide = false
                                        descendant.Massless = true
                                    end
                                end

                                task.spawn(function()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        holdPart.HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(foodHamburger, character_2)
                                    end)
                                end)

                                task.wait(0.02)

                                task.spawn(function()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        holdPart.DropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(foodHamburger, CFrame.new(0, 5000, 0), Vector3.zero)
                                    end)
                                end)
                            end
                        else
                            task.spawn(function()
                                pcall(function()
                                    spawnToyRemoteFunction2:InvokeServer("FoodHamburger", humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -12, 0), Vector3.zero)
                                end)
                            end)

                            task.wait(0.1)
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(0.02)
                end
            end)
        else
            pcall(function()
                local v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                local foodHamburger = v18 and v18:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger")

                if foodHamburger and DestroyToy then
                    DestroyToy:FireServer(foodHamburger)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

v8:CreateToggle({

        AutoSit = arg
        local creatureBlobman = nil

        while AutoSit do
            local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if not creatureBlobman or not creatureBlobman.Parent then
                local v18 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

                if v18 then
                    creatureBlobman = v18:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
                end

                if not creatureBlobman or not creatureBlobman.Parent then
                    local v19 = FindPlot_AntiStun()

                    if v19 then
                        creatureBlobman = v19:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
                    end
                end

                if not creatureBlobman or not creatureBlobman.Parent then
                    SpawnBlobman_AntiStun()

                    for i = 1, 100 do
                        if v18 then
                            creatureBlobman = v18:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
                        end

                        if not creatureBlobman or not creatureBlobman.Parent then
                            local v19 = FindPlot_AntiStun()

                            if v19 then
                                creatureBlobman = v19:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
                            end
                        end

                        if creatureBlobman and creatureBlobman.Parent then
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            end

            if humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and creatureBlobman then
                local vehicleSeat = creatureBlobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

                if vehicleSeat and vehicleSeat.Occupant == nil then
                    vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid_2)
                end
            end

            task.wait()
        end
    end,
})

v9:CreateSection("ループキック")

v9:CreateToggle({
    Name = "ループキック",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "lkickowner_toggle",
    Callback = function(loopKickOwnership)
        _G.LoopKickOwnership = loopKickOwnership

        if loopKickOwnership then
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.LoopKickOwnership do
                    local v19 = getCF()
                    local targetPlayerName = targetName ~= "" and targetName or _G.TargetPlayerName

                    if targetPlayerName ~= "" then
                        local v20 = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPlayerName)
                        local humanoidRootPart_ = v20 and v20.Character and v20.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if v20 and humanoidRootPart_ and isValid(v20) and not isInSafeZone(v20) and changePriority(2) then
                            for i = 1, 50 do
                                startFloat()
                                claimNetOwner(humanoidRootPart_)

                                if isValid(v20) and not isInSafeZone(v20) and _G.LoopKickOwnership and not checkPlayerOwner(v20) and humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude <= 500 then
                                    task.wait()

                                    if humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                        tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)), 2)
                                    else
                                        tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)), 2)
                                    end

                                    continue
                                end

                                destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
                                task.wait()
                                applySkyVelocity(humanoidRootPart_)
                                break
                            end

                            changePriority(0)
                        end
                    end

                    if v19 then
                        tpSimple(v19)
                    end

                    task.wait(0.2)
                end

                stopFloat()
                local v19 = getCF()

                if v19 then
                    tpSimple(v19)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

v9:CreateSection("スパム")

v9:CreateToggle({
    Name = "スパムキック（プレミアム👑）",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "TaskWaitToggle",
    Callback = function(arg)
        if arg then
            StopAll()
            local targetPlayerName = targetName ~= "" and targetName or _G.TargetPlayerName
            local v19 = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPlayerName)
            currentTarget = v19

            if not currentTarget then
                Rayfield:Notify({
                    Title = "Error",
                    Content = "Targetを選択してください",
                    Duration = 3,
                })

                return
            end

            isSpamActive = true
            SetNoclip(true)

            Rayfield:Notify({
                Title = "Spam Kick (task.wait)",
                Content = "Started on " .. currentTarget.Name,
                Duration = 3,
            })

            local thread3 = task.spawn(function()
                local v20 = currentTarget
                if not v20 then
                    StopAll()
                    return
                end
                local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
                local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoidRootPart_ then
                    StopAll()
                    return
                end
                savedPosition = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
                dragging = false
                grabStartTime = 0
                checkStartTime = 0
                local flag9 = false

                while isSpamActive do
                    local v21 = Players:FindFirstChild(v20.Name)

                    if v21 then
                        local character_3 = localPlayer.Character
                        local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_3 and character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local v22 = humanoidRootPart_2
                        local character_4 = v21.Character
                        local humanoidRootPart_3 = character_4 and character_4:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local humanoid_2 = character_4 and character_4:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                        if humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and v22 then
                            if not flag9 then
                                flag9 = true

                                local thread3 = task.spawn(function()
                                    while isSpamActive and humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoidRootPart_3.Parent do
                                        if humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoidRootPart_3.Parent then
                                            DoDestroyGrabLine(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                        end

                                        task.wait()

                                        if humanoidRootPart_3 and humanoidRootPart_3.Parent then
                                            SetNetworkOwnerForTarget(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                        end

                                        task.wait()
                                    end
                                end)

                                currentSpamTask = thread3
                            end

                            if dragging then
                                v22.CFrame = savedPosition
                                local cFrame2 = savedPosition * CFrame.new(0, 17, 0)
                                v22.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                v22.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                                if currentBodyPosition and currentBodyPosition.Parent then
                                    currentBodyPosition.Position = cFrame2.Position

                                    if currentBodyGyro then
                                        currentBodyGyro.CFrame = cFrame2
                                    end
                                else
                                    CreateBodies(humanoidRootPart_3, cFrame2.Position)
                                end

                                if checkStartTime > 0 and tick() - checkStartTime > 0.3 then
                                    if (humanoidRootPart_3.Position - cFrame2.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                                        dragging = false
                                        grabStartTime = 0
                                        checkStartTime = 0
                                        CleanupBodies()
                                        v22.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_3.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                                    else
                                        local now = tick()
                                        checkStartTime = now
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                v22.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_3.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                                CleanupBodies()
                                checkStartTime = 0

                                pcall(function()
                                    SetNetworkOwnerForTarget(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                    SetNetworkOwnerForTarget(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                    DoDestroyGrabLine(humanoidRootPart_3)
                                end)

                                v22.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                v22.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                                if grabStartTime == 0 then
                                    local now = tick()
                                    grabStartTime = now
                                end

                                if tick() - grabStartTime > 0.35 then
                                    dragging = true
                                    grabStartTime = 0
                                    local now = tick()
                                    checkStartTime = now
                                    local n = savedPosition * CFrame.new(0, 17, 0)
                                    CreateBodies(humanoidRootPart_3, n.Position)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            dragging = false
                            grabStartTime = 0
                            checkStartTime = 0
                            CleanupBodies()
                            flag9 = false
                        end

                        task.wait()
                    else
                        CleanupBodies()
                        flag9 = false
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end

                CleanupBodies()
                SetNoclip(false)
            end)

            currentMainTask = thread3
        elseif isSpamActive then
            StopAll()
        end
    end,
})

local v19 = v9:CreateToggle({
    Name = "ブロブマンキル",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "AutoBlobKillToggle",
    Callback = function(arg)
        if arg then
            local targetPlayerName = targetName ~= "" and targetName or _G.TargetPlayerName

            if targetPlayerName == "" then
                Rayfield:Notify({
                    Title = "Warning",
                    Content = "ドロップダウンからターゲットを選択してください",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Image = "skull",
                })

                if not isSettingSelf then
                    isSettingSelf = true
                    AutoBlobKillToggleObj:Set(false)
                    isSettingSelf = false
                end

                return
            end

            BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(targetPlayerName)

            if not BlobmanKill.selectedPlayer then
                Rayfield:Notify({
                    Title = "Warning",
                    Content = "ターゲットプレイヤーが見つかりません",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Image = "skull",
                })

                if not isSettingSelf then
                    isSettingSelf = true
                    AutoBlobKillToggleObj:Set(false)
                    isSettingSelf = false
                end

                return
            end

            BlobmanKill.isSelectedKill = true
            BlobmanKill.StartSelectedKillLoop()

            Rayfield:Notify({
                Title = "Activated",
                Content = "ブロブマンキル起動: " .. targetPlayerName,
                Duration = 2,
                Image = "skull",
            })
        else
            StopSelectedKill()

            Rayfield:Notify({
                Title = "Deactivated",
                Content = "ブロブマンキル停止",
                Duration = 2,
                Image = "skull",
            })
        end
    end,
})

AutoBlobKillToggleObj = v19
v9:CreateSection("ブロブマン削除")

v9:CreateToggle({
    Name = "ブラックホールブロブマン",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "ShurikenAntiKickToggle",
    Callback = function(shurikenAntiKick)
        _G.ShurikenAntiKick = shurikenAntiKick

        local function fn25()
            local tbl4 = {}
            local v20 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

            if v20 then
                table.insert(tbl4, v20)
            end

            local v21, v22 = getHomePlot(localPlayer)

            if v21 and v22 then
                table.insert(tbl4, v22)
            end

            local menuToys2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
            local destroyToy2 = menuToys2 and ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")

            if destroyToy2 then
                local v23, v24, v25 = ipairs(tbl4)
                local v26 = v25
                local v27 = v23
                local v28 = v24

                while true do
                    local v29, v30 = v27(v28, v26)
                    if v29 == nil then
                        break
                    end

                    for i, child in ipairs(v30:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "AntiKick" or child.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then
                            pcall(function()
                                destroyToy2:FireServer(child)
                            end)
                        end
                    end

                    v26 = v29
                end
            end
        end

        if shurikenAntiKick then
            task.spawn(function()
                local function fn26(arg, arg2)
                    if not arg then
                        return
                    end
                    local stickyPart_ = arg:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1)
                    if not stickyPart_ or not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
                        return
                    end
                    local v20 = getChar()
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = v20 and getChar().HumanoidRootPart
                    if not humanoidRootPart_ then
                        return
                    end
                    local soundPart = arg:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                    local v21 = soundPart or stickyPart_

                    if v21 then
                        pcall(function()
                            setNetOwner:FireServer(v21, humanoidRootPart_.CFrame)
                        end)
                    end

                    task.wait(0.05)
                    if not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
                        return
                    end

                    pcall(function()
                        stickyPart:FireServer(stickyPart_, arg2, CFrame.new(0, -3, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(90)))
                    end)

                    for i, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "Pyramid" then
                            child.CanTouch = false
                            child.CanCollide = false
                            child.CanQuery = false
                            child.Transparency = 0

                            if not child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
                                Instance.new("Highlight", child).FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                            end
                        elseif child.Name == "Main" then
                            child.CanTouch = false
                            child.CanCollide = false
                            child.CanQuery = false
                            child.Transparency = 0

                            if not child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then
                                Instance.new("Highlight", child).FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                            end
                        elseif child:IsA("BasePart") then
                            child.CanTouch = false
                            child.CanCollide = false
                            child.CanQuery = false
                            child.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end

                local function fn27(arg)
                    local now = tick()

                    while not canSpawn.Value do
                        if not _G.ShurikenAntiKick or tick() - now > 5 then
                            return nil
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end

                    local v20 = getChar()
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = v20 and getChar().HumanoidRootPart

                    if humanoidRootPart_ then
                        pcall(function()
                            spawnToy:InvokeServer(arg, humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
                        end)
                    end

                    local v21 = nil
                    local now2 = tick()

                    while tick() - now2 < 2 and _G.ShurikenAntiKick do
                        local v22 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        local v23, v24 = getHomePlot(localPlayer)
                        local tbl4 = {}

                        if v22 then
                            table.insert(tbl4, v22)
                        end

                        if v23 and v24 then
                            table.insert(tbl4, v24)
                        end

                        for i, v25 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                            local v26 = v25:FindFirstChild(arg)

                            if v26 then
                                v21 = v26
                                v26.Name = "AntiKick"
                                break
                            end

                            continue
                        end

                        if v21 then
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end

                    return v21
                end

                while _G.ShurikenAntiKick do
                    local v20 = Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
                    local v21 = v20 or Players:FindFirstChild(_G.TargetPlayerName)
                    local v22 = getChar()
                    local humanoid_2 = v22 and getChar():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    if v21 and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 then
                        local v23 = getBlobOrGucciPart(v21)

                        if v23 and v23.Parent then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local NinjaShuriken = fn27("NinjaShuriken")

                                if NinjaShuriken then
                                    fn26(NinjaShuriken, v23)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                fn25()
            end)
        else
            fn25()
        end
    end,
})

v10:CreateToggle({
    Name = "スピンオーラ",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "sipin aura",
    Callback = function(enabled)
        OrbitConfig.Enabled = enabled

        if orbitConnection then
            orbitConnection:Disconnect()
            orbitConnection = nil
        end

        if enabled then
            local grabEvents_3 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
            local setNetworkOwner2 = grabEvents_3 and ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
            local tbl4 = {}

            local connection3 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
                local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoidRootPart_ then
                    return
                end
                OrbitConfig.AngleOffset = OrbitConfig.AngleOffset + deltaTime * OrbitConfig.Speed
                local tbl5 = {}

                for i, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                        local humanoidRootPart_2 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if humanoidRootPart_2 and (humanoidRootPart_2.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude < 50 then
                            table.insert(tbl5, {root = humanoidRootPart_2, char = player.Character, player = player})
                        end
                    end
                end

                local n = #tbl5
                if n == 0 then
                    return
                end
                local now = os.clock()

                for i, v20 in ipairs(tbl5) do
                    local root = v20.root
                    local humanoid_2 = v20.char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local player = v20.player

                    if setNetworkOwner2 and player then
                        local v21 = tbl4[player.UserId]
                        local n2 = v21 or 0

                        if now - n2 > 0.5 then
                            pcall(function()
                                setNetworkOwner2:FireServer(root, root.CFrame)
                            end)

                            tbl4[player.UserId] = now
                        end
                    end

                    local n2 = OrbitConfig.AngleOffset + i * (math.pi * 2 / n)
                    local n3 = Vector3.new(math.cos(n2), 0, math.sin(n2)) * OrbitConfig.Radius
                    root.Velocity = (humanoidRootPart_.Position + n3 + Vector3.new(0, OrbitConfig.Height, 0) - root.Position) * 15

                    if humanoid_2 then
                        humanoid_2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
                        humanoid_2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
                    end
                end
            end)

            orbitConnection = connection3
        end
    end,
})

v10:CreateSection("シールド設定")

v10:CreateToggle({
    Name = "テレキネシスシールド",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "TelekinesisShieldToggle",
    Callback = function(arg)
        telekinesisShieldActive = arg

        if arg then
            local thread3 = task.spawn(function()
                if not setNetOwner then
                    warn("SetNetworkOwner remote event not found.")
                    return
                end
                local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
                overlapParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

                while telekinesisShieldActive do
                    local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart_ then
                        overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character_2}
                        local partBoundsInRadius = Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(humanoidRootPart_.Position, 60, overlapParams)

                        for i, partBoundsInRadiu in ipairs(partBoundsInRadius) do
                            if not telekinesisShieldActive then
                                break
                            end

                            if partBoundsInRadiu:IsA("BasePart") and not partBoundsInRadiu.Anchored then
                                local assemblyLinearVelocity = ((partBoundsInRadiu.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Unit + Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)).Unit * 100

                                pcall(function()
                                    setNetOwner:FireServer(partBoundsInRadiu, partBoundsInRadiu.CFrame)
                                    partBoundsInRadiu.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity
                                    partBoundsInRadiu.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10))
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
            end)

            telekinesisShieldTask = thread3
        elseif telekinesisShieldTask then
            task.cancel(telekinesisShieldTask)
            telekinesisShieldTask = nil
        end
    end,
})

v11:CreateToggle({
    Name = "キックオール(ネットワーク)",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "KickAllNetworkToggle",
    Callback = function(kickAllNetwork)
        _G.KickAllNetwork = kickAllNetwork

        if kickAllNetwork then
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.KickAllNetwork do
                    local v20 = getCF()
                    local v21, v22, v23 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
                    local v24 = v23
                    local v25 = v21
                    local v26 = v22

                    while true do
                        local v27, v28 = v25(v26, v24)
                        if v27 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        v24 = v27

                        if v28 ~= localPlayer and v28.Character and not isInSafeZone(v28) and not isFloating(v28) then
                            local humanoidRootPart_ = v28.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                            if humanoidRootPart_ then
                                for i = 1, 50 do
                                    startFloat()
                                    claimNetOwner(humanoidRootPart_)

                                    if not isInSafeZone(v28) and not isFloating(v28) and _G.KickAllNetwork and not checkPlayerOwner(v28) and humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude <= 500 then
                                        task.wait()

                                        if humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                            tpSimple(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)))
                                        else
                                            tpSimple(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)))
                                        end

                                        continue
                                    end

                                    applySkyVelocity(humanoidRootPart_)
                                    destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
                                    break
                                end

                                v24 = v27
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if v20 then
                        tpSimple(v20)
                    end

                    task.wait(0.2)
                end

                stopFloat()
                local v20 = getCF()

                if v20 then
                    tpSimple(v20)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

v11:CreateSection("ブリングオール")

v11:CreateToggle({
    Name = "ブリングオール",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "BringAll_Toggle",
    Callback = function(bringAll)
        _G.BringAll = bringAll

        if bringAll then
            local v20 = getCF()
            if not v20 then
                return
            end
            local cframe = CFrame.lookAt(v20.Position + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0.1), v20.Position)
            Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = cframe

            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.BringAll do
                    freezeCam.CFrame = cframe
                    Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
                    Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = freezeCam
                    local v21, v22, v23 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
                    local v24 = v23
                    local v25 = v21
                    local v26 = v22

                    while true do
                        local v27, v28 = v25(v26, v24)
                        if v27 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        v24 = v27
                        local character_2 = v28.Character
                        local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local humanoid_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local ragdolled = humanoid_2 and humanoid_2:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                        local flag9 = false

                        if v28 ~= localPlayer and character_2 and (not table.find(friendsList, v28.Name) or not _G.WhitelistFriends3) and not isInSafeZone(v28) and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_.Velocity.Magnitude < 20 then
                            flag9 = true
                        end

                        if flag9 and humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 and ragdolled then
                            _G.TP_Priority = 2

                            for i = 1, 50 do
                                if not _G.BringAll then
                                    break
                                end
                                startFloat()
                                claimNetOwner(humanoidRootPart_)

                                if not checkPlayerOwner(v28) then
                                    task.wait()

                                    if humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                        tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)), 2)
                                    else
                                        tpTo(CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)), 2)
                                    end

                                    continue
                                end

                                if not ragdolled.Value and v28:DistanceFromCharacter(v20.Position) > 10 then
                                    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = v20
                                end

                                local bringBody = humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("BringBody")
                                local v29 = bringBody

                                if not bringBody then
                                    local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", humanoidRootPart_)
                                    v29 = bodyPosition
                                    bodyPosition.Name = "BringBody"
                                    bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                    bodyPosition.D = 5000
                                    bodyPosition.P = 1500000
                                end

                                v29.Position = v20.Position
                                break
                            end

                            v24 = v27
                            _G.TP_Priority = 0
                        end
                    end

                    tpTo(CFrame.new(527, 123, -376))
                    task.wait()
                end

                if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                    Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                end

                Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

                if not _G.NoclipToggle then
                    stopFloat()
                end

                if v20 then
                    tpTo(v20)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

_G.KillAll = false
killAllFloatConn = nil
killAllIsFloating = false

startKillAllFloating = function()
    if not _G.KillAll then
        return
    end

    if not killAllFloatConn then
        killAllIsFloating = false

        local connection3 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if killAllIsFloating == false and localPlayer.Character then
                for k, child in pairs(localPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                        child.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        killAllFloatConn = connection3
    end
end

toggleKillAllNoclip = function()
    if killAllFloatConn then
        killAllFloatConn:Disconnect()
        killAllFloatConn = nil
    end

    killAllIsFloating = true
end

killAllTeleport = function(arg, arg2)
    local v20 = getChar()

    if v20 and typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = v20.HumanoidRootPart
        local humanoid_2 = v20:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if arg2 then
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = arg + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        else
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.Rotation + arg.Position
        end

        humanoid_2.Sit = false
    end
end

isPlayerInSafeZoneKillAll = function(arg)
    if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg:IsA("Player") and arg:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and arg.InPlot.Value then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

checkPlayerKillAll = function(arg)
    if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg ~= localPlayer and arg.Character then
        local humanoidRootPart_ = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid_2 = arg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoidRootPart_ and humanoid_2 and humanoid_2.Health > 0 and not isPlayerInSafeZoneKillAll(arg) then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

snowShipKillAll = function(arg)
    if arg and typeof(arg) == "Instance" then
        local v20 = getChar()

        if v20 and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg.Position) <= 30 then
            setNetOwner:FireServer(arg, lookAt(v20.HumanoidRootPart.Position, arg.Position))
        end
    end
end

getWorkspacePlayers = function()
    return game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()
end

checkNetworkOwnershipKillAll = function(arg)
    if arg and arg.Character and arg.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        local partOwner = arg.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
        if partOwner and partOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

v11:CreateSection("キルオール")

v11:CreateToggle({
    Name = "キルオール",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "KillAllToggle",
    Callback = function(killAll)
        _G.KillAll = killAll

        if _G.KillAll then
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.KillAll do
                    local v20 = getCF()

                    if v20 then
                        local v21 = getWorkspacePlayers()
                        local v22, v23, v24 = pairs(v21)
                        local v25 = v24
                        local v26 = v22
                        local v27 = v23

                        while true do
                            local v28, v29 = v26(v27, v25)
                            if v28 == nil then
                                break
                            end
                            v25 = v28

                            if checkPlayerKillAll(v29) then
                                local character_2 = v29.Character
                                local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                AmericanHRP = humanoidRootPart_
                                local humanoid_2 = character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                                if AmericanHRP and humanoid_2 then
                                    for i = 1, 50 do
                                        if not _G.KillAll then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        startKillAllFloating()
                                        snowShipKillAll(AmericanHRP)

                                        if checkPlayerKillAll(v29) and not checkNetworkOwnershipKillAll(v29) and AmericanHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude <= 500 then
                                            task.wait()

                                            if AmericanHRP.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                                killAllTeleport(CFrame.new(AmericanHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)), false)
                                            else
                                                killAllTeleport(CFrame.new(AmericanHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)), false)
                                            end

                                            humanoid_2.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                                            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                                            humanoid_2.Jump = true
                                            humanoid_2.Sit = false
                                            continue
                                        end

                                        CreateSkyVelocity(AmericanHRP)
                                        destroyLine:FireServer(AmericanHRP)
                                        break
                                    end

                                    v25 = v28
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        killAllTeleport(v20, true)
                    end

                    task.wait(0.2)
                end

                toggleKillAllNoclip()
            end)
        else
            toggleKillAllNoclip()
        end
    end,
})
