v11:CreateButton({
    Name = "キックオール(ラグ)(PC)(プレミアム👑)",
    Callback = function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local n = lagHeight == "Heaven" and 1000000000 or 35
            startLag()
            task.wait(1)
            local v20 = getNonWhitelisted()
            if #v20 == 0 then
                stopLag()
                return
            end
            local v21 = getChar()
            local humanoidRootPart_ = v21 and v21:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart_ then
                stopLag()
                return
            end
            local tbl4 = {}

            for i, v22 in ipairs(v20) do
                local character_2 = v22.Character
                local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart_2 then
                    table.insert(tbl4, {player = v22, root = humanoidRootPart_2})
                end
            end

            for i, v22 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                pcall(function()
                    humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = v22.root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 5)
                    humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)

                task.wait(0.2)

                if grabEvents then
                    local setNetworkOwner2 = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")

                    if setNetworkOwner2 then
                        pcall(function()
                            setNetworkOwner2:FireServer(v22.root, v22.root.CFrame)
                        end)
                    end
                end

                task.wait()
            end

            local n2 = math.pi * 2 / #tbl4

            for i, v22 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                local n3 = (i - 1) * n2
                local n4 = math.cos(n3) * 40
                local n5 = math.sin(n3) * 40

                pcall(function()
                    v22.root.CFrame = CFrame.new(n4, n, n5)
                    v22.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)

                local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
                bodyPosition.P = 50000000000
                bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(n4, n, n5)
                bodyPosition.Parent = v22.root

                task.delay(2, function()
                    pcall(function()
                        bodyPosition:Destroy()
                    end)
                end)

                task.wait()
            end

            for i = 1, 8 do
                for i2, v22 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                    if grabEvents then
                        local createGrabLine2 = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
                        local destroyGrabLine2 = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")

                        if createGrabLine2 and destroyGrabLine2 then
                            pcall(function()
                                createGrabLine2:FireServer(v22.root, CFrame.new(0, 1000000000, 0))
                                task.wait()
                                destroyGrabLine2:FireServer(v22.root)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end

                task.wait(0.3)
            end

            task.wait(6)
            stopLag()
        end)
    end,
})

local grabEvents_3 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
local thread3 = nil
local flag9 = false

local function fn25()
    local tbl4 = {}

    for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            table.insert(tbl4, player)
        end
    end

    return tbl4
end

local function fn26(arg)
    if not grabEvents_3 then
        return
    end
    local setNetworkOwner2 = grabEvents_3:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")

    if setNetworkOwner2 and arg then
        pcall(function()
            setNetworkOwner2:FireServer(arg, arg.CFrame)
        end)
    end
end

local function fn27(arg, arg2)
    if not arg or not arg2 then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        arg.CFrame = arg2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 5)
        arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

local function fn28(arg)
    if not grabEvents_3 then
        return
    end
    local createGrabLine2 = grabEvents_3:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
    local destroyGrabLine2 = grabEvents_3:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")
    if not createGrabLine2 or not destroyGrabLine2 then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        createGrabLine2:FireServer(arg, CFrame.new(0, 1000000000, 0))
        task.wait()
        destroyGrabLine2:FireServer(arg)
    end)
end

local function fn29()
    if flag9 then
        return
    end
    flag9 = true

    thread3 = coroutine.create(function()
        if not grabEvents_3 then
            return
        end
        local createGrabLine2 = grabEvents_3:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
        if not createGrabLine2 then
            return
        end

        while flag9 do
            local spawnLocation = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
            local spawn_ = spawnLocation or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

            if not spawn_ then
                local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
                spawn_ = character_2 and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            end

            if spawn_ then
                local n = math.random(-1000000000, 1000000000)
                local n2 = math.random(-1000000000, 1000000000)

                local tbl4 = {
                    CFrame.new(n, 0, n2),
                    CFrame.new(-n, 0, -n2),
                    CFrame.new(n, 0, -n2),
                    CFrame.new(-n, 0, n2),
                }

                for k, v20 in pairs(tbl4) do
                    createGrabLine2:FireServer(spawn_, v20)
                end
            end

            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    coroutine.resume(thread3)
end

local function fn30()
    flag9 = false

    if thread3 then
        coroutine.close(thread3)
        thread3 = nil
    end
end

v11:CreateButton({
    Name = "キックオール(ラグ)(モバイル)(プレミアム👑)",
    Callback = function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local n = lagHeight == "Heaven" and 1000000000 or 35
            fn29()
            task.wait(1)
            local v20 = fn25()

            if #v20 == 0 then
                fn30()

                Rayfield:Notify({
                    Title = "Destroy",
                    Content = "対象プレイヤーがいません",
                    Duration = 3,
                })

                return
            end

            local character_2 = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart_ then
                fn30()
                return
            end
            local tbl4 = {}

            for i, v21 in ipairs(v20) do
                local character_3 = v21.Character
                local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_3 and character_3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart_2 then
                    table.insert(tbl4, {player = v21, hrp = humanoidRootPart_2})
                end
            end

            for i, v21 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                fn27(humanoidRootPart_, v21.hrp)
                task.wait(0.2)
                fn26(v21.hrp)
                task.wait()
            end

            local n2 = math.pi * 2 / #tbl4

            for i, v21 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                local n3 = (i - 1) * n2
                local n4 = math.cos(n3) * 40
                local n5 = math.sin(n3) * 40

                pcall(function()
                    v21.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(n4, n, n5)
                    v21.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)

                local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1000000000, 1000000000, 1000000000)
                bodyPosition.P = 40000000
                bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(n4, n, n5)
                bodyPosition.Parent = v21.hrp

                task.delay(2, function()
                    pcall(function()
                        bodyPosition:Destroy()

                    Title = "Lag",
                    Content = "ラグ処理を自動停止しました",
                    Duration = 2,
                })
            end)

            for i = 1, 8 do
                for i2, v21 in ipairs(tbl4) do
                    fn28(v21.hrp)
                end

                task.wait(0.3)
            end

            Rayfield:Notify({
                Title = "Destroy",
                Content = "完了！9秒後にラグは自動停止します。",
                Duration = 2,
            })
        end)
    end,
})

    Name = "スパムライン",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "Spam line Toggle",
    Callback = function(lagServerActive)
        Settings.LagServerActive = lagServerActive

        if lagServerActive then
            task.spawn(function()
                if not CreateGrabLine then
                    Rayfield:Notify({
                        Title = "Error",
                        Content = "CreateGrabLine remote event was not found. Game might be patched or unsupported.",
                        Duration = 5,
                    })

                    return
                end

                while Settings.LagServerActive do
                    for i = 1, Settings.Lag_Intensity do
                        if not Settings.LagServerActive then
                            break
                        end

                        for i2, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if player.Character then
                                local torso = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                                local upperTorso = torso or player.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

                                if upperTorso then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        CreateGrabLine:FireServer(upperTorso, upperTorso.CFrame)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

v12:CreateSection("クレイジーライン設定")

v12:CreateToggle({
    Name = "クレイジーライン",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "CrazyLineToggle",
    Callback = function(crazyLine)
        _G.CrazyLine = crazyLine

        if _G.CrazyLine then
            task.spawn(function()
                local v20, v21, v22, v23, v24, v25, v26, torso, upperTorso, cframe, n
                n = 1

                while true do
                    if n == 1 then
                        v20 = getCreateGrabLineEvent()

                        if v20 then
                            n = 2
                        else
                            n = 13
                        end
                    elseif n == 2 then
                        if _G.CrazyLine then
                            n = 3
                        else
                            n = 12
                        end
                    elseif n == 3 then
                        v21, v22, v23 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
                        v24 = v23
                        n = 4
                    elseif n == 4 then
                        v25, v26 = v21(v22, v24)

                        if v25 == nil then
                            n = 2
                        else
                            n = 5
                        end
                    elseif n == 5 then
                        v24 = v25

                        if _G.CrazyLine then
                            n = 6
                        else
                            n = 2
                        end
                    elseif n == 6 then
                        if v26 ~= localPlayer and v26.Character then
                            n = 7
                        else
                            n = 11
                        end
                    elseif n == 7 then
                        torso = v26.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        upperTorso = torso

                        if torso then
                            n = 9
                        else
                            n = 8
                        end
                    elseif n == 8 then
                        upperTorso = v26.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                        n = 9
                    elseif n == 9 then
                        if upperTorso then
                            n = 10
                        else
                            n = 11
                        end
                    elseif n == 10 then
                        cframe = CFrame.new(0.12640380859375, 0.9606337547302246, -0.5000009536743164, 0.9985212683677673, 0, -0.05436277016997337, -6.4805472099749295e-09, 1, -1.1903301100346653e-07, 0.05436277016997337, 5.960464477539063e-08, 0.9985212683677673)

                        pcall(function()
                            v20:FireServer(upperTorso, cframe)
                        end)

                        n = 11
                    elseif n == 11 then
                        task.wait()
                        n = 4
                    elseif n == 12 then
                        return
                    elseif n == 13 then
                        return
                    end

v13:CreateDropdown({
    Name = "部位",
    Options = {"背後", "左", "右", "正面"},
    CurrentOption = {"背後"},
    MultipleOptions = false,
    Flag = "TPBehavior",
    Callback = function(arg)
        local v21 = arg[1]
        local str = v21 or "背後"

        if str ~= "背後" and str ~= "Behind" then
            if str ~= "正面" and str ~= "Front" then
                if str ~= "右" and str ~= "Right" then
                    if str ~= "左" and str ~= "Left" then
                        _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection = "Behind"
                    else
                        _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection = "Left"
                    end
                else
                    _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection = "Right"
                end
            else
                _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection = "Front"
            end
        else
            _G.PlayerToTeleportDirection = "Behind"
        end
    end,
})

        _G.LoopPlayerTP = loopPlayerTP

        if loopPlayerTP then
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.LoopPlayerTP do
                    local v21 = Players:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayerToTeleport)

                    if v21 and v21.Character and not IsPlayerKickingWithBlobman() then
                        local humanoidRootPart_ = v21.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if humanoidRootPart_ then
                            teleportplayerfunctionoffset(humanoidRootPart_.CFrame)
                        end
                    elseif not v21 then
                        _G.LoopPlayerTP = false

                        if LoopTeleportToggle then
                            LoopTeleportToggle:Set(false)
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

                if character_2 then
                    character_2.Parent = playersCharFolder

            local function fn32()

v16:CreateToggle({
    Name = "海をきれいにする",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "DynamicWaterToggle",
    Callback = function(arg)
        if arg then
            local terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
            local terrain2 = terrain or Workspace.Terrain
            local map2 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local alwaysHereTweenedObjects = map2 and map2:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
            local ocean = alwaysHereTweenedObjects and alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
            local object = ocean and ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
            local objectModel = object and object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

            if objectModel and terrain2 then
                for i, child in ipairs(objectModel:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Part") then
                        local size = child.Size
                        local cFrame2 = child.CFrame
                        terrain2:FillRegion(Region3.new(cFrame2.Position - size / 2, cFrame2.Position + size / 2):ExpandToGrid(4), 4, Enum.Material.Water)
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if _G.SuperSpeed then
        local v24 = getChar()
        local humanoid_2 = v24 and v24:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoid_2 and v24:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local humanoidRootPart_ = v24.HumanoidRootPart
            local cFrame2 = v24.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            local moveDirection = humanoid_2.MoveDirection
            local multiplier = getgenv().Multiplier
            local n = multiplier or 0.15
            humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2 + moveDirection * n
        end
    end
end)

UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if infJumpEnabled then
        local v24 = getChar()
        local humanoid_2 = v24 and v24:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoid_2 then
            humanoid_2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        end
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel and grabZoomEnabled then
        grabZoomValue = grabZoomValue + input.Position.Z * 5
        local n = math.clamp(grabZoomValue, 1, 300)
        grabZoomValue = n
    end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if Settings.Visuals.EnableFOVAdjust then
        local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera

        if currentCamera2 then
            currentCamera2.FieldOfView = Settings.Visuals.CameraFOV
        end
    end
end)

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
    monitorPlayer(player)
    setupPlayer(player)
    task.wait(1)

    if TargetDropdown then
        TargetDropdown:Refresh(getDropdownList())
    end

    if TeleportDropdown then
        TeleportDropdown:Refresh(getTPDropdownList())
    end
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    targetSpawnTimes[player.Name] = nil
    removePlayer(player)
    task.wait(1)

    if TargetDropdown then
        TargetDropdown:Refresh(getDropdownList())
    end

    if TeleportDropdown then
        TeleportDropdown:Refresh(getTPDropdownList())
    end

    if kickStatus[player.UserId] == "Kicked" then
        Rayfield:Notify({
            Title = "キックしました",
            Content = player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")",
            Image = 3944680095,
            Duration = 5,
        })
    else
        Rayfield:Notify({
            Title = "退出しました",
            Content = player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")",
            Image = 448345998,
            Duration = 5,
        })
    end

    kickStatus[player.UserId] = nil
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if _G.KickAura then
            for k, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if not _G.KickAura then
                    break
                end

                if CheckPlayerAurasKick(player) then
                    local character_2 = player.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart_ = character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart_ and character_2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") and SNOWshipPlayerKick(player) then
                        applySkyVelocity(humanoidRootPart_)
                        destroyLine:FireServer(humanoidRootPart_)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1)

    pcall(function()
        sendChat("Doraemon hub made by gimini⭐⭐⭐")
    end)
end)
